Kimi:

这是一份架构清晰、痛点精准的治理需求文档。我将以架构师视角分批次为你提供专业反馈，重点聚焦于技术可行性、实施路径和风险控制。

📋 批次一：整体架构与治理策略评估

1. 文档核心洞察（What I See）

你的系统正在经历”有机增长→架构治理”的关键转型期： - 现状：从20个脚本野蛮生长到43+脚本，符合任何活跃系统的熵增规律 - 本质矛盾：文档化治理与自动化运行的”双轨制”张力——你们在用”重文档+轻代码”的方式运行一个准产品级系统 - 关键风险：Token硬约束下的”治理过载”——过度治理本身会消耗宝贵的AI对话预算

2. 架构建议：轻量级联邦制（而非中央集权）

你提出的”五大指挥部”架构是正确的方向，但我建议采用“轻联邦 + 强契约”模式：

┌─────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  daily_asset_runner.py (联邦调度器)     │ ← 只负责"何时跑"，不负责"怎么跑"
├─────────┬─────────┬─────────┬──────────┤
│ Defense │Intel    │Business │Management│Library
│ (防御)  │(情报)   │(业务)   │(治理)    │(资产库)
└────┬────┴────┬────┴────┬────┴────┬─────┘
     │         │         │         │
     └─────────┴────┬────┴─────────┘
                    ↓
          ┌─────────────────┐
          │ OpenClaw Gateway │ ← 统一契约层
          │ (技能白名单管理)  │
          └─────────────────┘

关键修正： - daily_asset_runner 应该只调度，不干预业务逻辑。不要在调度器里做依赖检测，这会导致调度器本身成为单点瓶颈。 - 依赖检测应在各指挥部内部通过__dependencies__元数据在导入时自动完成，而非运行时。

3. 约定治理：警惕”文档完美主义”陷阱

你提出的”一令一源”（每个约定一个权威文档）是P0级需求，但存在Token悖论：

我的建议：建立三级索引架构

docs/COMMAND_INDEX.md (仅索引，无内容) ← 常驻上下文
├── SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md
├── DEEP_INSIGHT_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md  
├── ...
└── QUICK_REF.md (纯表格速查，紧急情况用)

仅在执行特定任务时，通过代码逻辑动态加载对应协议，而非将所有协议都塞进AI上下文。

4. Token优化：从”节流”到”架构级分流”

你的baseline checker缓存策略（30分钟）还不够，建议实施三级触发机制：

关键代码模式：

# 在基类中实现自动分流
class BaseComponent:
    def write_operation(func):
        def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
            if not self._baseline_cached:
                self._run_baseline_check()
            return func(self, *args, **kwargs)
        return wrapper

📋 批次二：技术实施关键决策（待确认问题回应）

针对附录中的封闭式问题，我的建议如下：

Q1: 技术栈确认 ✅

Python + SQLite + CLI 是正确选择。但建议增加： - Typer（取代argparse）用于CLI构建，代码量减少60% - Pydantic 用于所有数据结构的Schema验证，取代手写JSON校验 - Rich 用于终端可视化（表格/进度条/树形图），提升UX

Q3: 全局语义索引策略 🔍

推荐”SQLite全文检索 + 轻量向量”混合方案，而非重型向量库：

# 技术选型
├── 文本检索: SQLite FTS5 (内置，零依赖)
├── 语义匹配: 本地嵌入模型 (all-MiniLM-L6-v2, 80MB)
└── 向量存储: SQLite + numpy (1000文档内无需FAISS)

理由：你们的文档量级（200+）在SQLite的甜蜜区内，引入Chroma/FAISS会增加部署复杂度且违反”数据本地化”约束。

Q4: 告警通道建议 📢

采用”本地日志 + 飞书机器人”双轨： - 开发阶段：仅本地日志（避免告警疲劳） - 生产阶段：飞书机器人（利用现有OpenClaw生态） - 避免短信/邮件：增加外部依赖，违反”无人值守但可控”原则

Q5: 半闭环转正优先级 ✅

强烈建议聚焦”涌现匹配”与”压力测试”： - 这两个是最靠近商业化的资产（见MKT-001） - 技术债务清理应在有明确付费场景下进行，否则只是技术 vanity

📋 批次三：两份关键方案的技术路径细化

涌现匹配系统（EM-001~005）

当前半闭环的技术卡点： 1. 公开数据采集的法律灰区：LinkedIn/GitHub的ToS禁止自动化抓取 2. NLP分析的幻觉风险：价值观信号提取需要极高精度的提示工程

务实路径建议：

72小时压力测试系统（PT-001~005）

关键安全红线： - PT-REQ-002（生理监控）：Apple Watch/小米手环的实时数据接入技术难度极高（需iOS/Android原生开发），建议降级为手动定时输入 + 异常阈值告警 - PT-REQ-004（视频索引）：OpenCV+FFmpeg的方案可行，但建议先做音频转录文本索引（成本更低，检索更准）

最小可行产品（MVP）：

手机扫码 → 飞书多维表格录入观察 → 自动计算评分 → Streamlit查看雷达图

Kimi:

我将为你提供一套可直接在Claw环境运行的完整代码实施方案，按照文档需求编号顺序，涵盖从基础设施到业务系统的全部层级。

由于内容庞大，我将分基础设施层→治理协议层→业务系统层→监控安全层四个批次交付，每批次提供可直接复制到.py文件运行的完整代码。

🔧 批次一：基础设施与元数据治理（P0级）

1.1 核心基类升级（STD-002）

文件: core/base_component_v2.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
BaseComponent V2.0 - 全系统元数据标准基类
需求覆盖: STD-002, EFF-001, ROL-001, SEC-003
"""

import json
import hashlib
import inspect
import sqlite3
from datetime import datetime
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Any, Callable
from functools import wraps
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import git
from contextlib import contextmanager

# === 元数据规范 (STD-002) ===
@dataclass
class ComponentMeta:
    """组件元数据标准结构"""
    __version__: str = "1.0.0"
    __dependencies__: List[str] = None
    __category__: str = "library"  # defense/intelligence/business/management/library
    __author__: str = "system"
    __last_updated__: str = None
    __baseline_required__: bool = True  # 是否需要基线检查
    
    def __post_init__(self):
        if self.__dependencies__ is None:
            self.__dependencies__ = []
        if self.__last_updated__ is None:
            self.__last_updated__ = datetime.now().isoformat()

class BaseComponent:
    """
    全系统组件基类 - 所有脚本必须继承此类
    提供：元数据管理、基线检查缓存、Git快照、性能监控
    """
    
    # 类级配置（子类必须覆盖）
    META = ComponentMeta()
    
    # 实例级状态
    _baseline_cache: Dict[str, Any] = {}
    _cache_timestamp: Optional[datetime] = None
    _cache_duration: int = 1800  # 30分钟缓存 (EFF-001)
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root).resolve()
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "runtime.db"
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._init_db()
        
        # 初始化时自动注册到索引
        self._register_component()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        """初始化SQLite运行时库"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_registry (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT UNIQUE,
                    version TEXT,
                    category TEXT,
                    author TEXT,
                    last_run TIMESTAMP,
                    run_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    avg_duration_ms REAL,
                    dependencies TEXT,  -- JSON
                    meta_hash TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS baseline_cache (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    component_name TEXT,
                    cache_data TEXT,  -- JSON
                    created_at TIMESTAMP,
                    expires_at TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS git_snapshots (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    commit_hash TEXT,
                    stash_ref TEXT,
                    trigger_reason TEXT,
                    created_at TIMESTAMP,
                    auto_cleanup BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS token_budget (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    date TEXT UNIQUE,
                    daily_limit INTEGER DEFAULT 100000,
                    consumed INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    last_updated TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            
            conn.commit()
    
    def _register_component(self):
        """自动注册组件到系统索引"""
        meta_dict = asdict(self.META)
        meta_hash = hashlib.md5(
            json.dumps(meta_dict, sort_keys=True).encode()
        ).hexdigest()[:12]
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO component_registry 
                (name, version, category, author, dependencies, meta_hash)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                self.__class__.__name__,
                self.META.__version__,
                self.META.__category__,
                self.META.__author__,
                json.dumps(self.META.__dependencies__),
                meta_hash
            ))
            conn.commit()
    
    # === 基线检查按需触发 (EFF-001) ===
    def baseline_check(self, force: bool = False) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """
        智能基线检查 - 带缓存机制
        仅当需要写入操作或缓存过期时执行完整检查
        """
        now = datetime.now()
        cache_key = self.__class__.__name__
        
        # 检查内存缓存
        if not force and self._cache_timestamp:
            if (now - self._cache_timestamp).seconds < self._cache_duration:
                return self._baseline_cache.get(cache_key, {"status": "cached", "level": "L0"})
        
        # 检查数据库缓存
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT cache_data, expires_at FROM baseline_cache 
                WHERE component_name = ? AND expires_at > ?
            """, (cache_key, now.isoformat()))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if row and not force:
                cache_data = json.loads(row[0])
                self._baseline_cache[cache_key] = cache_data
                self._cache_timestamp = now
                return cache_data
        
        # 执行完整基线检查（L2级）
        result = self._run_full_baseline_check()
        
        # 写入缓存
        expires = datetime.fromtimestamp(now.timestamp() + self._cache_duration)
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO baseline_cache 
                (component_name, cache_data, created_at, expires_at)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (cache_key, json.dumps(result), now.isoformat(), expires.isoformat()))
            conn.commit()
        
        self._baseline_cache[cache_key] = result
        self._cache_timestamp = now
        return result
    
    def _run_full_baseline_check(self) -> Dict[str, Any]:
        """完整基线检查逻辑 - 子类可覆盖"""
        return {
            "status": "healthy",
            "level": "L2",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "checks": {
                "workspace_exists": self.workspace.exists(),
                "memory_dir_exists": (self.workspace / "memory").exists(),
                "docs_dir_exists": (self.workspace / "docs").exists(),
                "git_repo_valid": self._check_git_health()
            }
        }
    
    def _check_git_health(self) -> bool:
        """检查Git仓库健康状态"""
        try:
            repo = git.Repo(self.workspace)
            return not repo.bare
        except:
            return False
    
    # === Git自动快照 (ROL-001) ===
    def create_git_snapshot(self, reason: str = "auto") -> str:
        """执行Git stash创建恢复点"""
        try:
            repo = git.Repo(self.workspace)
            
            # 添加所有变更
            repo.git.add(".")
            
            # 创建stash
            stash_msg = f"auto-snapshot-{reason}-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}"
            repo.git.stash("save", stash_msg)
            
            # 获取stash引用
            stash_list = repo.git.stash("list").split("\n")
            stash_ref = None
            for line in stash_list:
                if stash_msg in line:
                    stash_ref = line.split(":")[0]
                    break
            
            # 记录到数据库
            commit_hash = repo.head.commit.hexsha[:8] if repo.head else "none"
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO git_snapshots (commit_hash, stash_ref, trigger_reason, created_at)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (commit_hash, stash_ref, reason, datetime.now().isoformat()))
                conn.commit()
            
            return stash_ref or "unknown"
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._log_error(f"Git snapshot failed: {e}")
            return f"error-{str(e)}"
    
    def rollback_to_snapshot(self, stash_ref: Optional[str] = None) -> bool:
        """一键回滚到指定stash或最近一次"""
        try:
            repo = git.Repo(self.workspace)
            
            if stash_ref is None:
                # 获取最近一次自动快照
                with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                    cursor = conn.execute("""
                        SELECT stash_ref FROM git_snapshots 
                        WHERE auto_cleanup = 1
                        ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1
                    """)
                    row = cursor.fetchone()
                    if row:
                        stash_ref = row[0]
            
            if stash_ref and stash_ref != "unknown":
                repo.git.stash("pop", stash_ref)
                return True
            return False
            
        except Exception as e:
            self._log_error(f"Rollback failed: {e}")
            return False
    
    # === Token预算熔断 (TOK-001, MON-002) ===
    def check_token_budget(self, estimated_cost: int = 1000) -> bool:
        """检查Token预算，超支时拒绝执行"""
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT consumed, daily_limit FROM token_budget WHERE date = ?
            """, (today,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                # 初始化今日预算
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO token_budget (date, consumed, last_updated)
                    VALUES (?, 0, ?)
                """, (today, datetime.now().isoformat()))
                conn.commit()
                current = 0
                limit = 100000
            else:
                current, limit = row
            
            # 检查是否超支（预留30%缓冲）
            if (current + estimated_cost) > (limit * 0.7):
                self._log_error(f"Token budget warning: {current}/{limit}")
                return False
            
            return True
    
    def consume_token(self, cost: int):
        """记录Token消耗"""
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE token_budget 
                SET consumed = consumed + ?, last_updated = ?
                WHERE date = ?
            """, (cost, datetime.now().isoformat(), today))
            conn.commit()
    
    # === 性能监控 ===
    @contextmanager
    def monitor_execution(self):
        """上下文管理器：监控执行时间和状态"""
        start = datetime.now()
        status = "success"
        try:
            yield self
        except Exception as e:
            status = f"failed-{str(e)}"
            raise
        finally:
            duration = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
            
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE component_registry 
                    SET last_run = ?, run_count = run_count + 1, avg_duration_ms = ?
                    WHERE name = ?
                """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), duration, self.__class__.__name__))
                conn.commit()
    
    # === 工具方法 ===
    def _log_error(self, msg: str):
        """错误日志记录"""
        log_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "error.log"
        with open(log_path, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"{datetime.now().isoformat()} - {self.__class__.__name__}: {msg}\n")
    
    def get_dependency_graph(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
        """获取组件依赖图（用于INT-002）"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT name, dependencies FROM component_registry
            """)
            graph = {}
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                name, deps = row
                try:
                    dep_list = json.loads(deps) if deps else []
                except:
                    dep_list = []
                graph[name] = dep_list
            return graph

# === 装饰器：写入操作保护 ===
def write_operation(func: Callable) -> Callable:
    """装饰器：在写入操作前强制执行基线检查和Git快照"""
    @wraps(func)
    def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
        # 1. 检查Token预算
        if not self.check_token_budget(estimated_cost=2000):
            raise RuntimeError("Token budget exceeded, operation blocked")
        
        # 2. 按需基线检查（L2级）
        if self.META.__baseline_required__:
            baseline = self.baseline_check(force=False)
            if baseline.get("status") != "healthy":
                raise RuntimeError(f"Baseline check failed: {baseline}")
        
        # 3. 创建Git快照
        snapshot = self.create_git_snapshot(reason=f"write-{func.__name__}")
        
        try:
            with self.monitor_execution():
                result = func(self, *args, **kwargs)
                return result
        except Exception as e:
            # 失败时自动回滚（可选）
            # self.rollback_to_snapshot(snapshot)
            raise
    
    return wrapper

# === 使用示例 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 测试基类
    class TestComponent(BaseComponent):
        META = ComponentMeta(
            __version__="1.0.0",
            __dependencies__=["core.base_component_v2"],
            __category__="test",
            __author__="tester",
            __baseline_required__=True
        )
        
        @write_operation
        def create_file(self, content: str):
            test_file = self.workspace / "test_output.txt"
            test_file.write_text(content, encoding="utf-8")
            return str(test_file)
    
    # 运行测试
    comp = TestComponent()
    print("基线检查结果:", comp.baseline_check())
    print("依赖图:", comp.get_dependency_graph())
    
    # 测试写入
    try:
        result = comp.create_file("Hello System")
        print(f"写入成功: {result}")
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"写入失败: {e}")

1.2 全局索引系统实现（INT-003, SCH-001~003）

文件: core/workspace_indexer.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Workspace全局索引系统
需求覆盖: INT-003, EFF-003, SCH-001~003, DOC-002
功能：秒级文件/函数/约定检索，语义索引，符号表生成
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import hashlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Set, Tuple
import re
import ast

class WorkspaceIndexer:
    """
    全局索引器 - 支持秒级检索和语义查询
    索引范围：所有.md/.py/.json文件
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root).resolve()
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "workspace_index.db"
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._init_schema()
    
    def _init_schema(self):
        """初始化索引数据库Schema"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 文件索引表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS file_index (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    path TEXT UNIQUE,
                    filename TEXT,
                    extension TEXT,
                    size_bytes INTEGER,
                    modified_time TIMESTAMP,
                    content_hash TEXT,
                    title TEXT,
                    summary TEXT,
                    tags TEXT,  -- JSON数组
                    key_sections TEXT  -- JSON：段落标题→行号映射
                )
            """)
            
            # 符号表（函数/类/约定）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS symbol_index (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    symbol_name TEXT,
                    symbol_type TEXT,  -- function/class/protocol/variable
                    file_path TEXT,
                    line_number INTEGER,
                    docstring TEXT,
                    signature TEXT,
                    dependencies TEXT  -- JSON：该符号依赖的其他符号
                )
            """)
            
            # 约定/协议索引
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS convention_index (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT UNIQUE,  -- 如 SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL
                    file_path TEXT,
                    anchor_line INTEGER,
                    description TEXT,
                    triggers TEXT,  -- JSON：触发条件
                    output_format TEXT,
                    version TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            # 全文搜索虚拟表（FTS5）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE VIRTUAL TABLE IF NOT EXISTS content_fts USING fts5(
                    path, content, tokenize='porter'
                )
            """)
            
            # 任务报告索引（A-manyige/汇报/）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS report_index (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    file_path TEXT UNIQUE,
                    report_date TEXT,
                    task_id TEXT,
                    status TEXT,
                    summary TEXT,
                    keywords TEXT,
                    related_conventions TEXT  -- JSON
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_symbol_name ON symbol_index(symbol_name)
            """)
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE INDEX IF NOT EXISTS idx_file_ext ON file_index(extension)
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def full_indexing(self) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """
        全量索引重建
        返回统计信息
        """
        stats = {"files": 0, "symbols": 0, "conventions": 0, "reports": 0}
        
        # 清空旧索引
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM file_index")
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM symbol_index")
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM convention_index")
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM content_fts")
            conn.execute("DELETE FROM report_index")
            conn.commit()
        
        # 遍历Workspace
        for file_path in self.workspace.rglob("*"):
            if file_path.is_file() and file_path.suffix in ['.md', '.py', '.json']:
                try:
                    self._index_single_file(file_path)
                    stats["files"] += 1
                except Exception as e:
                    print(f"索引失败 {file_path}: {e}")
        
        # 扫描约定协议
        stats["conventions"] = self._index_conventions()
        
        # 扫描汇报文件
        stats["reports"] = self._index_reports()
        
        return stats
    
    def _index_single_file(self, file_path: Path):
        """索引单个文件"""
        rel_path = str(file_path.relative_to(self.workspace))
        content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        content_hash = hashlib.md5(content.encode()).hexdigest()
        stat = file_path.stat()
        
        # 提取关键信息
        title = self._extract_title(content, file_path.suffix)
        sections = self._extract_sections(content, file_path.suffix)
        tags = self._extract_tags(content)
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 插入文件索引
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO file_index 
                (path, filename, extension, size_bytes, modified_time, content_hash, title, summary, tags, key_sections)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                rel_path,
                file_path.name,
                file_path.suffix,
                stat.st_size,
                datetime.fromtimestamp(stat.st_mtime).isoformat(),
                content_hash,
                title,
                content[:200] + "..." if len(content) > 200 else content,
                json.dumps(tags),
                json.dumps(sections)
            ))
            
            # 更新FTS索引
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO content_fts (path, content)
                VALUES (?, ?)
            """, (rel_path, content))
            
            # Python文件：提取符号
            if file_path.suffix == '.py':
                self._index_python_symbols(conn, rel_path, content)
            
            conn.commit()
    
    def _extract_title(self, content: str, ext: str) -> str:
        """提取文档标题"""
        if ext == '.md':
            # Markdown H1
            match = re.search(r'^#\s+(.+)$', content, re.MULTILINE)
            if match:
                return match.group(1).strip()
        elif ext == '.py':
            # 模块docstring
            match = re.search(r'^(?:"""|\'\'\')(.+?)(?:"""|\'\'\')', content, re.DOTALL)
            if match:
                first_line = match.group(1).strip().split('\n')[0]
                return first_line[:100]
        return "Untitled"
    
    def _extract_sections(self, content: str, ext: str) -> Dict[str, int]:
        """提取关键段落标题及行号"""
        sections = {}
        lines = content.split('\n')
        
        for i, line in enumerate(lines, 1):
            if ext == '.md' and line.startswith('#'):
                title = line.lstrip('#').strip()
                sections[title] = i
            elif ext == '.py' and line.strip().startswith('def '):
                func_name = line.strip().split('def ')[1].split('(')[0]
                sections[f"func:{func_name}"] = i
            elif ext == '.py' and line.strip().startswith('class '):
                class_name = line.strip().split('class ')[1].split('(')[0].rstrip(':')
                sections[f"class:{class_name}"] = i
        
        return sections
    
    def _extract_tags(self, content: str) -> List[str]:
        """提取标签（YAML frontmatter或特殊标记）"""
        tags = []
        
        # 尝试提取YAML frontmatter
        if content.startswith('---'):
            try:
                frontmatter = content.split('---')[1]
                if 'tags:' in frontmatter:
                    tags_match = re.search(r'tags:\s*\[(.*?)\]', frontmatter)
                    if tags_match:
                        tags = [t.strip().strip('"\'') for t in tags_match.group(1).split(',')]
            except:
                pass
        
        # 提取特殊标记如 #TODO #P0 #约定
        tags += re.findall(r'#[\w\u4e00-\u9fa5]+', content)
        
        return list(set(tags))
    
    def _index_python_symbols(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection, file_path: str, content: str):
        """使用AST提取Python符号"""
        try:
            tree = ast.parse(content)
            
            for node in ast.walk(tree):
                if isinstance(node, ast.FunctionDef):
                    docstring = ast.get_docstring(node) or ""
                    signature = f"def {node.name}({ast.unparse(node.args) if hasattr(ast, 'unparse') else '...'})"
                    
                    conn.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO symbol_index 
                        (symbol_name, symbol_type, file_path, line_number, docstring, signature)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (node.name, "function", file_path, node.lineno, docstring[:200], signature))
                    
                elif isinstance(node, ast.ClassDef):
                    docstring = ast.get_docstring(node) or ""
                    
                    conn.execute("""
                        INSERT INTO symbol_index 
                        (symbol_name, symbol_type, file_path, line_number, docstring, signature)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (node.name, "class", file_path, node.lineno, docstring[:200], f"class {node.name}"))
                    
        except SyntaxError:
            pass  # 忽略语法错误文件
    
    def _index_conventions(self) -> int:
        """扫描docs/下的协议文档"""
        count = 0
        docs_dir = self.workspace / "docs"
        
        if not docs_dir.exists():
            return 0
        
        for md_file in docs_dir.glob("*PROTOCOL*.md"):
            content = md_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            rel_path = str(md_file.relative_to(self.workspace))
            
            # 提取协议名称（如 SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL_V1.0）
            name_match = re.search(r'#\s*(.+?)\n', content)
            if name_match:
                name = name_match.group(1).strip()
                
                # 提取版本
                version_match = re.search(r'版本[:：]\s*([Vv\d\.]+)', content)
                version = version_match.group(1) if version_match else "unknown"
                
                # 提取描述（第一个段落）
                desc_match = re.search(r'\n\n(.+?)\n\n', content)
                description = desc_match.group(1)[:200] if desc_match else ""
                
                with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                    conn.execute("""
                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO convention_index 
                        (name, file_path, anchor_line, description, version)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (name, rel_path, 1, description, version))
                    conn.commit()
                count += 1
        
        return count
    
    def _index_reports(self) -> int:
        """扫描A-manyige/汇报/目录"""
        count = 0
        report_dir = self.workspace / "A-manyige" / "汇报"
        
        if not report_dir.exists():
            return 0
        
        for md_file in report_dir.glob("*.md"):
            content = md_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
            rel_path = str(md_file.relative_to(self.workspace))
            
            # 提取任务ID（如 TASK-2026-001）
            task_match = re.search(r'TASK-[\d-]+', content)
            task_id = task_match.group(0) if task_match else "unknown"
            
            # 提取日期
            date_match = re.search(r'\d{4}-\d{2}-\d{2}', md_file.name)
            report_date = date_match.group(0) if date_match else "unknown"
            
            # 提取状态
            status_match = re.search(r'状态[：:]\s*(\w+)', content)
            status = status_match.group(1) if status_match else "unknown"
            
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT OR REPLACE INTO report_index 
                    (file_path, report_date, task_id, status, summary, keywords)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    rel_path, 
                    report_date, 
                    task_id, 
                    status,
                    content[:150] + "...",
                    json.dumps(self._extract_tags(content))
                ))
                conn.commit()
            count += 1
        
        return count
    
    # === 查询接口 ===
    
    def search_files(self, keyword: str, ext: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """全文检索文件"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            
            if ext:
                sql = """
                    SELECT f.* FROM file_index f
                    JOIN content_fts c ON f.path = c.path
                    WHERE content_fts MATCH ? AND f.extension = ?
                    LIMIT 20
                """
                cursor = conn.execute(sql, (keyword, ext))
            else:
                sql = """
                    SELECT f.* FROM file_index f
                    JOIN content_fts c ON f.path = c.path
                    WHERE content_fts MATCH ?
                    LIMIT 20
                """
                cursor = conn.execute(sql, (keyword,))
            
            return [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    
    def find_symbol(self, symbol_name: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """精确查找符号定义位置"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM symbol_index WHERE symbol_name = ? LIMIT 1
            """, (symbol_name,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
    
    def get_convention(self, name: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取约定协议详情"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM convention_index WHERE name LIKE ?
            """, (f"%{name}%",))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            return dict(row) if row else None
    
    def list_reports(self, status: Optional[str] = None, date_from: Optional[str] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """列任务报告"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
            
            sql = "SELECT * FROM report_index WHERE 1=1"
            params = []
            
            if status:
                sql += " AND status = ?"
                params.append(status)
            if date_from:
                sql += " AND report_date >= ?"
                params.append(date_from)
            
            sql += " ORDER BY report_date DESC"
            
            cursor = conn.execute(sql, params)
            return [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    
    def get_dependency_graph(self) -> Dict[str, List[str]]:
        """获取组件依赖图"""
        # 从symbol_index中分析import关系
        graph = {}
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT path, content FROM file_index WHERE extension = '.py'")
            
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                path, content = row
                imports = re.findall(r'(?:from|import)\s+([\w\.]+)', content)
                graph[path] = list(set(imports))
        
        return graph

# === CLI接口 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="Workspace Indexer")
    parser.add_argument("--rebuild", action="store_true", help="重建全量索引")
    parser.add_argument("--search", type=str, help="搜索关键词")
    parser.add_argument("--symbol", type=str, help="查找符号")
    parser.add_argument("--convention", type=str, help="查找约定协议")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    indexer = WorkspaceIndexer()
    
    if args.rebuild:
        stats = indexer.full_indexing()
        print(f"索引完成: {stats}")
    
    if args.search:
        results = indexer.search_files(args.search)
        for r in results:
            print(f"[{r['extension']}] {r['path']} - {r['title']}")
    
    if args.symbol:
        result = indexer.find_symbol(args.symbol)
        if result:
            print(f"符号 {result['symbol_name']} 定义在 {result['file_path']}:{result['line_number']}")
        else:
            print("未找到符号")
    
    if args.convention:
        result = indexer.get_convention(args.convention)
        if result:
            print(f"约定 {result['name']} (v{result['version']})")
            print(f"位置: {result['file_path']}:{result['anchor_line']}")
            print(f"描述: {result['description']}")

1.3 约定索引与协议实现（CMD-001~006）

文件: core/command_index_manager.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
约定索引管理器 - 实现"一令一源"治理
需求覆盖: STD-001, CMD-001~006
"""

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Optional, List, Dict
from datetime import datetime

@dataclass
class CommandProtocol:
    """约定协议数据结构"""
    cmd_id: str           # 如 CMD-001
    name: str             # 如 SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL
    version: str          # 如 V1.0
    file_path: str        # docs/SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md
    description: str      # 简短描述
    triggers: List[str]   # 触发条件
    output_format: str    # 输出格式规范
    related_cmds: List[str]  # 关联命令
    priority: str         # P0/P1/P2

class CommandIndexManager:
    """
    约定索引管理器
    维护 docs/COMMAND_INDEX.md 的SQL后端
    """
    
    INDEX_FILE = "docs/COMMAND_INDEX.md"
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "command_index.db"
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS command_registry (
                    cmd_id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT UNIQUE,
                    version TEXT,
                    file_path TEXT,
                    description TEXT,
                    triggers TEXT,  -- JSON
                    output_format TEXT,
                    related_cmds TEXT,  -- JSON
                    priority TEXT,
                    last_verified TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def register_command(self, protocol: CommandProtocol):
        """注册约定到索引"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO command_registry 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                protocol.cmd_id,
                protocol.name,
                protocol.version,
                protocol.file_path,
                protocol.description,
                json.dumps(protocol.triggers),
                protocol.output_format,
                json.dumps(protocol.related_cmds),
                protocol.priority,
                datetime.now().isoformat()
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 同步更新Markdown索引文件
        self._sync_to_markdown()
    
    def get_command(self, cmd_id_or_name: str) -> Optional[CommandProtocol]:
        """获取约定详情"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM command_registry 
                WHERE cmd_id = ? OR name = ?
            """, (cmd_id_or_name, cmd_id_or_name))
            
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row:
                return CommandProtocol(
                    cmd_id=row[0],
                    name=row[1],
                    version=row[2],
                    file_path=row[3],
                    description=row[4],
                    triggers=json.loads(row[5]),
                    output_format=row[6],
                    related_cmds=json.loads(row[7]),
                    priority=row[8]
                )
            return None
    
    def check_compliance(self, cmd_name: str, context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """检查操作是否符合约定（蓝军审计用）"""
        cmd = self.get_command(cmd_name)
        if not cmd:
            return {"compliant": False, "error": f"Unknown command: {cmd_name}"}
        
        issues = []
        
        # 检查触发条件
        if "required_context" in context:
            missing = set(cmd.triggers) - set(context.get("triggers", []))
            if missing:
                issues.append(f"Missing triggers: {missing}")
        
        # 检查输出格式
        if "output" in context:
            if not self._validate_output_format(context["output"], cmd.output_format):
                issues.append(f"Output format violation: expected {cmd.output_format}")
        
        return {
            "compliant": len(issues) == 0,
            "issues": issues,
            "protocol_version": cmd.version
        }
    
    def _validate_output_format(self, output: str, format_spec: str) -> bool:
        """验证输出格式（简化版）"""
        # 实际实现应根据format_spec进行结构化验证
        return True
    
    def _sync_to_markdown(self):
        """将数据库同步到 COMMAND_INDEX.md"""
        index_path = self.workspace / self.INDEX_FILE
        index_path.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM command_registry ORDER BY priority, cmd_id
            """)
            
            lines = [
                "# 约定指令权威索引 (COMMAND INDEX)",
                "",
                "> 本文档由 command_index_manager.py 自动生成",
                f"> 最后更新: {datetime.now().isoformat()}",
                "",
                "## 索引规则",
                "",
                "- 每个约定唯一对应一个权威文档（一令一源）",
                "- 优先级：P0(阻断级) > P1(质量级) > P2(优化级)",
                "- 修改约定必须同步更新版本号并重新注册",
                "",
                "## 约定清单",
                "",
                "| 编号 | 名称 | 版本 | 优先级 | 文档路径 | 描述 |",
                "|------|------|------|--------|----------|------|"
            ]
            
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                cmd_id, name, version, file_path, desc, triggers, output_fmt, related, priority, _ = row
                lines.append(f"| {cmd_id} | {name} | {version} | {priority} | {file_path} | {desc[:30]}... |")
            
            lines.extend([
                "",
                "## 快速参考",
                "",
                "### P0级约定（必须遵守）",
                ""
            ])
            
            # 添加P0详情
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT * FROM command_registry WHERE priority = 'P0'")
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                cmd_id, name, version, file_path, desc, triggers, output_fmt, related, priority, _ = row
                lines.extend([
                    f"#### {name} ({cmd_id})",
                    "",
                    f"- **版本**: {version}",
                    f"- **文档**: [{file_path}]({file_path})",
                    f"- **描述**: {desc}",
                    f"- **触发条件**: {', '.join(json.loads(triggers))}",
                    f"- **输出格式**: {output_fmt}",
                    ""
                ])
            
            index_path.write_text("\n".join(lines), encoding="utf-8")

# === 具体协议实现示例 ===

class SilentModeProtocol:
    """
    静默模式协议实现 (CMD-001)
    文件: docs/SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md
    """
    
    PROTOCOL_ID = "CMD-001"
    
    @staticmethod
    def generate_pre_hibernation_checklist(context: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        生成前置条件检查清单 (C1-C6)
        TOK-004: 必须以最轻量方式完成
        """
        checklist = {
            "C1_token_budget": {
                "check": "token_available",
                "lightweight": True,  # 仅检查计数器，不读历史
                "passed": context.get("token_remaining", 0) > 5000
            },
            "C2_critical_tasks": {
                "check": "no_blocking_tasks",
                "lightweight": True,  # 仅检查标记位
                "passed": not context.get("has_p0_tasks", False)
            },
            "C3_git_status": {
                "check": "workspace_clean",
                "lightweight": True,  # 仅检查是否存在uncommitted标记文件
                "passed": context.get("git_clean", False)
            },
            "C4_data_persisted": {
                "check": "memory_synced",
                "lightweight": True,  # 检查时间戳文件存在性
                "passed": context.get("last_sync_within_1h", False)
            },
            "C5_external_deps": {
                "check": "api_available",
                "lightweight": True,  # 检查本地健康标记
                "passed": context.get("api_healthy", True)
            },
            "C6_emergency_contact": {
                "check": "contact_configured",
                "lightweight": True,  # 配置文件存在性
                "passed": context.get("emergency_config_exists", False)
            }
        }
        
        all_passed = all(item["passed"] for item in checklist.values())
        
        return {
            "can_enter_silent_mode": all_passed,
            "checklist": checklist,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "revision": "V1.0"
        }

class DeepInsightProtocol:
    """
    深度洞察协议实现 (CMD-002)
    强制执行 L1-L5 洞察
    """
    
    PROTOCOL_ID = "CMD-002"
    
    LEVELS = {
        "L1": "原始数据/事实提取",
        "L2": "模式识别/关联分析", 
        "L3": "异常检测/风险标记",
        "L4": "根因分析/机制推断",
        "L5": "战略建议/行动方案"
    }
    
    @classmethod
    def generate_insight_report(cls, raw_data: Dict, context: str) -> Dict:
        """
        生成L1-L5深度洞察报告
        STD-003: 必须在P0/P1闭环后10分钟内触发
        """
        report = {
            "context": context,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "levels": {}
        }
        
        # L1: 事实提取
        report["levels"]["L1"] = {
            "description": cls.LEVELS["L1"],
            "facts": cls._extract_facts(raw_data),
            "data_points": len(raw_data)
        }
        
        # L2: 模式识别
        report["levels"]["L2"] = {
            "description": cls.LEVELS["L2"],
            "patterns": cls._identify_patterns(raw_data),
            "correlations": cls._find_correlations(raw_data)
        }
        
        # L3: 异常检测
        report["levels"]["L3"] = {
            "description": cls.LEVELS["L3"],
            "anomalies": cls._detect_anomalies(raw_data),
            "risk_flags": cls._assess_risks(raw_data)
        }
        
        # L4: 根因分析
        report["levels"]["L4"] = {
            "description": cls.LEVELS["L4"],
            "root_causes": cls._analyze_root_causes(raw_data, report["levels"]["L3"]["anomalies"]),
            "mechanisms": cls._infer_mechanisms(raw_data)
        }
        
        # L5: 战略建议
        report["levels"]["L5"] = {
            "description": cls.LEVELS["L5"],
            "recommendations": cls._generate_recommendations(report),
            "action_items": cls._derive_actions(report),
            "priority_ranking": cls._rank_priorities(report)
        }
        
        # 元数据标记
        report["protocol"] = {
            "id": cls.PROTOCOL_ID,
            "version": "V1.0",
            "mandatory": True,
            "trigger_context": context
        }
        
        return report
    
    @staticmethod
    def _extract_facts(data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """L1: 提取原子事实"""
        facts = []
        for key, value in data.items():
            facts.append({
                "source_key": key,
                "value": str(value)[:100],  # 截断防溢出
                "type": type(value).__name__,
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
            })
        return facts
    
    @staticmethod
    def _identify_patterns(data: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """L2: 识别模式（简化版）"""
        patterns = []
        # 实现具体的模式识别逻辑
        if len(data) > 5:
            patterns.append("数据密度较高，存在聚合特征")
        return patterns
    
    @staticmethod
    def _find_correlations(data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """L2: 查找关联"""
        return []  # 占位实现
    
    @staticmethod
    def _detect_anomalies(data: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """L3: 异常检测"""
        anomalies = []
        for key, value in data.items():
            if isinstance(value, (int, float)):
                if value > 1000:  # 简单阈值
                    anomalies.append({
                        "field": key,
                        "value": value,
                        "reason": "exceeds_normal_threshold"
                    })
        return anomalies
    
    @staticmethod
    def _assess_risks(data: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """L3: 风险评估"""
        return ["risk_placeholder"]
    
    @staticmethod
    def _analyze_root_causes(data: Dict, anomalies: List) -> List[str]:
        """L4: 根因分析"""
        return [f"potential_root_cause_for_{a['field']}" for a in anomalies]
    
    @staticmethod
    def _infer_mechanisms(data: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """L4: 机制推断"""
        return []
    
    @staticmethod
    def _generate_recommendations(report: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """L5: 生成建议"""
        return ["基于L3异常，建议优先处理高风险项"]
    
    @staticmethod
    def _derive_actions(report: Dict) -> List[Dict]:
        """L5: 行动项"""
        return [{
            "action": "review_anomalies",
            "deadline": "immediate",
            "owner": "system"
        }]
    
    @staticmethod
    def _rank_priorities(report: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """L5: 优先级排序"""
        return ["P0", "P1", "P2"]

class HonestAuditProtocol:
    """
    诚实审计协议 (CMD-003)
    蓝军审计用 - 禁止输出"一切正常"
    """
    
    PROTOCOL_ID = "CMD-003"
    
    @staticmethod
    def conduct_audit(target_work: Dict, audit_scope: List[str]) -> Dict:
        """
        执行诚实审计
        必须输出至少3项指控（concerns）
        """
        concerns = []
        
        # 审计维度1：约定符合性
        if "conventions" in audit_scope:
            missing = HonestAuditProtocol._check_missing_conventions(target_work)
            if missing:
                concerns.append({
                    "category": "约定违反",
                    "severity": "high",
                    "description": f"缺少约定文档: {missing}",
                    "evidence": target_work.get("convention_refs", [])
                })
        
        # 审计维度2：Token效率
        if "token_efficiency" in audit_scope:
            if target_work.get("token_cost", 0) > 10000:
                concerns.append({
                    "category": "Token浪费",
                    "severity": "medium", 
                    "description": f"Token消耗过高: {target_work.get('token_cost')} > 10000",
                    "evidence": ["execution_log"]
                })
        
        # 审计维度3：完成度质疑
        if "completion" in audit_scope:
            if target_work.get("status") == "done" and not target_work.get("has_test_evidence"):
                concerns.append({
                    "category": "完成度欺诈",
                    "severity": "high",
                    "description": "标记为完成但缺少测试证据",
                    "evidence": ["status_label", "test_missing"]
                })
        
        # 强制填充：如果不足3项，进行深度质疑
        while len(concerns) < 3:
            concerns.append({
                "category": "深度质疑",
                "severity": "low",
                "description": f"审计项{len(concerns)+1}: 工作产物是否存在未声明的假设？",
                "evidence": ["inferred"]
            })
        
        return {
            "audit_id": f"AUDIT-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d-%H%M%S')}",
            "target": target_work.get("id"),
            "auditor": "blue_team",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "verdict": "BLOCKED" if any(c["severity"] == "high" for c in concerns) else "WARNING",
            "concerns": concerns,
            "pass_count": len([c for c in concerns if c["category"] != "深度质疑"]),
            "protocol_version": "V1.0"
        }
    
    @staticmethod
    def _check_missing_conventions(work: Dict) -> List[str]:
        """检查缺失的约定引用"""
        required = work.get("required_conventions", [])
        actual = work.get("convention_refs", [])
        return list(set(required) - set(actual))

# === 初始化示例 ===
def initialize_command_index():
    """初始化所有标准约定"""
    manager = CommandIndexManager()
    
    # 注册静默模式协议
    manager.register_command(CommandProtocol(
        cmd_id="CMD-001",
        name="SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL",
        version="V1.0",
        file_path="docs/SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md",
        description="无人值守模式进入与退出规范，含C1-C6轻量检查",
        triggers=["user_explicit_request", "token_threshold_alert", "time_based_scheduled"],
        output_format="JSON with checklist and timestamp",
        related_cmds=["CMD-002", "CMD-005"],
        priority="P0"
    ))
    
    # 注册深度洞察协议
    manager.register_command(CommandProtocol(
        cmd_id="CMD-002",
        name="DEEP_INSIGHT_PROTOCOL",
        version="V1.0", 
        file_path="docs/DEEP_INSIGHT_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md",
        description="强制执行L1-L5五层洞察，P0/P1闭环后10分钟内触发",
        triggers=["p0_closure", "p1_closure", "manual_request"],
        output_format="Structured JSON with L1-L5 keys",
        related_cmds=["CMD-003"],
        priority="P0"
    ))
    
    # 注册诚实审计协议
    manager.register_command(CommandProtocol(
        cmd_id="CMD-003",
        name="HONEST_AUDIT_PROTOCOL",
        version="V1.0",
        file_path="docs/HONEST_AUDIT_PROTOCOL_V1.0.md", 
        description="蓝军审计规范，禁止输出一切正常，至少3项指控",
        triggers=["p0_closure", "suspicious_quality", "random_sampling"],
        output_format="JSON with concerns array (min 3 items)",
        related_cmds=["CMD-002"],
        priority="P0"
    ))
    
    print("约定索引初始化完成")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    initialize_command_index()

2.1 涌现匹配系统转正（EM-001~005）

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
涌现匹配算法系统 V2.0 - 从半闭环到自运行
需求覆盖: EM-001~005, SEC-002, COO-002
技术栈: Python + SQLite + Streamlit (EM-REQ-005)
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import hashlib
import re
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import requests
from urllib.parse import quote_plus
import time

# 导入基类（假设core目录在路径中）
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))
from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent, write_operation, ComponentMeta

@dataclass
class CandidateProfile:
    """候选人档案结构（COO-002: 统一数据结构）"""
    candidate_id: str
    name: str
    email: Optional[str] = None
    linkedin_url: Optional[str] = None
    github_username: Optional[str] = None
    maimai_url: Optional[str] = None
    
    # 采集到的原始数据
    raw_data: Dict = None
    
    # L1-L3洞察标签（EM-REQ-002）
    l1_facts: Dict = None      # 事实提取
    l2_patterns: List = None   # 模式识别
    l3_signals: Dict = None    # 信号提取
    
    # 评分结果
    five_dimension_scores: Dict = None  # 五路图腾维度
    total_score: float = 0.0
    assessment_date: str = None
    
    # 合规标记（EM-REQ-004）
    ethics_verified: bool = False
    data_sources: List[str] = None  # 记录数据来源用于审计

class EmergenceMatchingSystem(BaseComponent):
    """
    涌现匹配系统 - 合伙人评估全自动化
    伦理红线：仅使用主动公开信息，私密数据自动丢弃
    """
    
    META = ComponentMeta(
        __version__="2.0.0",
        __dependencies__=["core.base_component_v2", "sqlite3"],
        __category__="intelligence",
        __author__="skeptor-7",
        __baseline_required__=True
    )
    
    # 五路图腾维度定义
    DIMENSIONS = {
        "red": {"name": "红莲淬火", "weight": 0.25, "keywords": ["抗压", "决断", "行动力"]},
        "blue": {"name": "蓝军审计", "weight": 0.20, "keywords": ["质疑", "验证", "系统性"]},
        "green": {"name": "生态构建", "weight": 0.20, "keywords": ["连接", "共赢", "长期主义"]},
        "gold": {"name": "价值沉淀", "weight": 0.20, "keywords": ["专业深度", "方法论", "传承"]},
        "purple": {"name": "涌现洞察", "weight": 0.15, "keywords": ["直觉", "创新", "跨界"]}
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        super().__init__(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / "data" / "emergence_matching.db"
        self.db_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._init_database()
        
        # 伦理审查日志（EM-REQ-004）
        self.ethics_log_path = self.workspace / "logs" / "ethics_audit"
        self.ethics_log_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    
    def _init_database(self):
        """初始化候选人数据库"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 候选人主表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS candidates (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT,
                    email TEXT,
                    created_at TIMESTAMP,
                    raw_data TEXT,  -- JSON
                    processed_data TEXT,  -- JSON
                    total_score REAL,
                    assessment_status TEXT,  -- pending/processing/completed/failed
                    ethics_verified BOOLEAN DEFAULT 0,
                    data_retention_until TIMESTAMP  -- 自动删除时间（GDPR合规）
                )
            """)
            
            # 数据来源审计表（EM-REQ-004）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS data_sources (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    candidate_id TEXT,
                    source_type TEXT,  # linkedin/github/maimai/manual
                    source_url TEXT,
                    accessed_at TIMESTAMP,
                    is_public BOOLEAN,  # 是否主动公开
                    content_hash TEXT,  # 完整性校验
                    FOREIGN KEY (candidate_id) REFERENCES candidates(id)
                )
            """)
            
            # 伦理审查日志表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ethics_audit_log (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    candidate_id TEXT,
                    check_time TIMESTAMP,
                    violation_found BOOLEAN,
                    violation_type TEXT,
                    action_taken TEXT,
                    reviewer TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            conn.commit()
    
    # === EM-REQ-001: 公开数据采集（合规版）===
    
    def collect_public_data(self, candidate: CandidateProfile, 
                           enable_github: bool = True,
                           enable_linkedin: bool = False,  # 默认关闭，需特殊处理
                           enable_maimai: bool = False) -> Dict:
        """
        采集公开数据 - 严格伦理审查
        EM-REQ-004: 自动审查闸门内嵌
        """
        collected = {}
        sources = []
        
        # GitHub采集（相对安全，公开API）
        if enable_github and candidate.github_username:
            try:
                github_data = self._fetch_github_data(candidate.github_username)
                if github_data:
                    collected["github"] = github_data
                    sources.append(("github", f"https://github.com/{candidate.github_username}", True))
            except Exception as e:
                self._log_ethics_event(candidate.candidate_id, "github_fetch_failed", str(e))
        
        # LinkedIn采集（高风险，默认关闭）
        if enable_linkedin and candidate.linkedin_url:
            # 警告：LinkedIn禁止自动化抓取，此处仅作示例
            # 实际应引导用户手动导出PDF后上传
            self._log_ethics_event(candidate.candidate_id, "linkedin_skipped", "自动化抓取违反ToS")
        
        # 脉脉采集（类似限制）
        if enable_maimai and candidate.maimai_url:
            self._log_ethics_event(candidate.candidate_id, "maimai_skipped", "需要登录态，无法自动化")
        
        # 记录数据来源
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            for source_type, url, is_public in sources:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO data_sources 
                    (candidate_id, source_type, source_url, accessed_at, is_public, content_hash)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    candidate.candidate_id,
                    source_type,
                    url,
                    datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    is_public,
                    hashlib.md5(json.dumps(collected).encode()).hexdigest()[:16]
                ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return collected
    
    def _fetch_github_data(self, username: str) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """获取GitHub公开数据（使用公开API，无需认证）"""
        try:
            # 公开API限制：60 requests/hour per IP
            headers = {"Accept": "application/vnd.github.v3+json"}
            
            # 用户基本信息
            user_resp = requests.get(
                f"https://api.github.com/users/{quote_plus(username)}",
                headers=headers,
                timeout=10
            )
            
            if user_resp.status_code != 200:
                return None
            
            user_data = user_resp.json()
            
            # 仓库列表（公开）
            repos_resp = requests.get(
                user_data["repos_url"],
                headers=headers,
                timeout=10
            )
            repos = repos_resp.json() if repos_resp.status_code == 200 else []
            
            # 分析提交活跃度（简化版：取最近5个repo的语言分布）
            languages = {}
            for repo in repos[:5]:
                lang = repo.get("language")
                if lang:
                    languages[lang] = languages.get(lang, 0) + 1
            
            return {
                "public_repos": user_data.get("public_repos", 0),
                "followers": user_data.get("followers", 0),
                "following": user_data.get("following", 0),
                "created_at": user_data.get("created_at"),
                "bio": user_data.get("bio", ""),
                "languages": languages,
                "top_repo_topics": self._extract_topics(repos[:3]),
                "account_age_days": (datetime.now() - datetime.fromisoformat(user_data.get("created_at", "").replace("Z", "+00:00"))).days if user_data.get("created_at") else 0
            }
            
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"GitHub fetch error: {e}")
            return None
    
    def _extract_topics(self, repos: List[Dict]) -> List[str]:
        """提取仓库主题标签"""
        topics = []
        for repo in repos:
            topics.extend(repo.get("topics", []))
        return list(set(topics))[:10]  # 去重，取前10
    
    # === EM-REQ-002: NLP内容分析 ===
    
    def analyze_content(self, candidate_id: str, raw_data: Dict) -> Dict:
        """
        NLP分析子系统 - 本地轻量实现
        关键词映射到五路图腾维度
        """
        # 合并所有文本数据
        all_text = ""
        if "github" in raw_data:
            all_text += raw_data["github"].get("bio", "") + " "
            all_text += " ".join(raw_data["github"].get("top_repo_topics", []))
        
        # 本地关键词匹配（轻量版NLP）
        dimension_scores = {}
        dimension_evidence = {}
        
        for dim_key, dim_info in self.DIMENSIONS.items():
            score = 0.0
            evidence = []
            
            for keyword in dim_info["keywords"]:
                count = all_text.lower().count(keyword.lower())
                if count > 0:
                    score += count * 0.1  # 简单加权
                    evidence.append(f"{keyword}({count})")
            
            # 归一化到0-100
            dimension_scores[dim_key] = min(score * 20, 100)
            dimension_evidence[dim_key] = evidence
        
        # L1-L3洞察生成
        l1_facts = {
            "word_count": len(all_text),
            "dimensions_detected": len([s for s in dimension_scores.values() if s > 0]),
            "primary_language": raw_data.get("github", {}).get("languages", {}).keys()[0] if raw_data.get("github", {}).get("languages") else "unknown"
        }
        
        l2_patterns = [
            "技术专注型" if dimension_scores.get("gold", 0) > 60 else "综合型",
            "社区活跃" if raw_data.get("github", {}).get("followers", 0) > 100 else "内敛型"
        ]
        
        l3_signals = {
            "leadership_potential": dimension_scores.get("red", 0) > 70 and dimension_scores.get("green", 0) > 50,
            "systematic_thinking": dimension_scores.get("blue", 0) > 60,
            "innovation_capacity": dimension_scores.get("purple", 0) > 50
        }
        
        # 计算总分（加权）
        total = sum(
            dimension_scores.get(k, 0) * v["weight"] 
            for k, v in self.DIMENSIONS.items()
        )
        
        # 更新数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE candidates 
                SET processed_data = ?, total_score = ?, assessment_status = ?
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (
                json.dumps({
                    "dimensions": dimension_scores,
                    "evidence": dimension_evidence,
                    "l1_facts": l1_facts,
                    "l2_patterns": l2_patterns,
                    "l3_signals": l3_signals
                }),
                total,
                "completed",
                candidate_id
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return {
            "candidate_id": candidate_id,
            "dimension_scores": dimension_scores,
            "total_score": total,
            "insights": {"l1": l1_facts, "l2": l2_patterns, "l3": l3_signals},
            "assessment_date": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    # === EM-REQ-003: 社交网络分析（简化版）===
    
    def analyze_network(self, candidate_id: str, connections_data: List[Dict]) -> Dict:
        """
        SNA模块 - 基于共同联系人
        由于数据限制，此处为结构化输入处理
        """
        if not connections_data:
            return {"network_score": 0, "diversity_index": 0}
        
        # 计算网络多样性（行业分布）
        industries = [c.get("industry", "unknown") for c in connections_data]
        unique_industries = len(set(industries))
        diversity = unique_industries / len(industries) if industries else 0
        
        # 中心性估算（连接数）
        centrality = len(connections_data)
        
        network_score = min((diversity * 50) + (centrality * 0.5), 100)
        
        return {
            "network_score": network_score,
            "diversity_index": diversity,
            "centrality": centrality,
            "complementarity_suggestions": self._suggest_complements(industries)
        }
    
    def _suggest_complements(self, industries: List[str]) -> List[str]:
        """基于现有网络推荐互补性伙伴"""
        # 简单规则：如果技术多，推荐商业；如果金融多，推荐产品
        suggestions = []
        if "technology" in industries or "software" in industries:
            suggestions.append("建议补充：商业拓展背景")
        if "finance" in industries:
            suggestions.append("建议补充：产品设计背景")
        return suggestions
    
    # === EM-REQ-004: 伦理审查自动化 ===
    
    def _log_ethics_event(self, candidate_id: str, event_type: str, details: str):
        """记录伦理审查事件"""
        log_file = self.ethics_log_path / f"ethics_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y-%W')}.log"
        with open(log_file, "a", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            f.write(f"{datetime.now().isoformat()} | {candidate_id} | {event_type} | {details}\n")
        
        # 同时写入数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO ethics_audit_log 
                (candidate_id, check_time, violation_found, violation_type, action_taken, reviewer)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                candidate_id,
                datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "violation" in event_type,
                event_type,
                "blocked" if "blocked" in event_type else "logged",
                "system_auto"
            ))
            conn.commit()
    
    @write_operation
    def verify_ethics_compliance(self, candidate_id: str) -> Dict:
        """
        伦理合规自动审查闸门
        检查：1.数据来源是否公开 2.是否涉及隐私信息 3.完整性校验
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 获取数据来源
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT source_type, source_url, is_public, content_hash 
                FROM data_sources WHERE candidate_id = ?
            """, (candidate_id,))
            sources = cursor.fetchall()
            
            violations = []
            
            for source_type, url, is_public, content_hash in sources:
                # 检查1：是否标记为公开
                if not is_public:
                    violations.append({
                        "type": "non_public_source",
                        "source": source_type,
                        "action": "data_quarantined"
                    })
                
                # 检查2：URL是否包含敏感路径（如/private, /settings等）
                if url and any(sensitive in url.lower() for sensitive in ["private", "settings", "admin", "internal"]):
                    violations.append({
                        "type": "suspicious_url_pattern",
                        "source": url,
                        "action": "review_required"
                    })
            
            # 更新候选人状态
            is_compliant = len(violations) == 0
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE candidates SET ethics_verified = ? WHERE id = ?
            """, (is_compliant, candidate_id))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 记录审查结果
        self._log_ethics_event(
            candidate_id, 
            "ethics_verified" if is_compliant else "ethics_violations_found",
            json.dumps(violations)
        )
        
        return {
            "candidate_id": candidate_id,
            "compliant": is_compliant,
            "violations": violations,
            "review_timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    # === EM-REQ-005: Web UI 数据录入 ===
    
    def generate_streamlit_ui_code(self) -> str:
        """
        生成Streamlit UI代码字符串
        用户可保存为 app.py 直接运行
        """
        ui_code = '''

page = st.sidebar.radio("功能导航", ["录入候选人", "查看评估", "伦理审计日志", "系统状态"])

    ''')
    
    for row in c.fetchall():

'''
        return ui_code
    
    @write_operation
    def export_ui_file(self):
        """导出UI文件到workspace"""
        ui_path = self.workspace / "apps" / "emergence_matching_ui.py"
        ui_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
