2.2 72小时压力测试系统转正（PT-001~005）

文件: systems/pressure_test_72h_system.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
72小时压力测试系统 V2.0 - 多终端协同与高安全监控
需求覆盖: PT-001~005, SEC-002, ROL-002, MON-001
技术栈: SQLite + WebSocket + OpenCV(视频索引)
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import asyncio
import websockets
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict
import hashlib
import cv2
import numpy as np
from concurrent.futures import ThreadPoolExecutor

import sys
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))
from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent, write_operation, ComponentMeta

@dataclass
class TestSubject:
    """测试参与者"""
    subject_id: str
    name: str
    age: int
    emergency_contact: str
    medical_notes: Optional[str] = None
    consent_signed: bool = False
    consent_timestamp: Optional[str] = None

@dataclass
class ObservationRecord:
    """观察记录"""
    record_id: str
    test_id: str
    observer_id: str
    timestamp: str
    task_phase: str  # 如 "T1-压力峰值", "T2-冲突模拟"
    
    # 三维九项数据
    dimension_red: Dict  # 红莲淬火：决断力/抗压力/目标感
    dimension_blue: Dict  # 蓝军审计：质疑力/反思度/逻辑性
    dimension_green: Dict  # 生态构建：共情力/连接力/修复力
    
    physiological_data: Optional[Dict] = None  # 心率等
    video_timestamp: Optional[float] = None  # 对应视频时间点

class PressureTestSystem(BaseComponent):
    """
    72小时压力测试管理系统
    安全红线：生理监控 + 自动熔断机制
    """
    
    META = ComponentMeta(
        __version__="2.0.0",
        __dependencies__=["core.base_component_v2", "sqlite3", "websockets"],
        __category__="defense",
        __author__="skeptor-7",
        __baseline_required__=True
    )
    
    # 生理安全红线（PT-REQ-002）
    SAFETY_THRESHOLDS = {
        "heart_rate_max": 180,  # 心率上限
        "heart_rate_min": 50,   # 心率下限（危险信号）
        "consecutive_hours_without_sleep": 20,  # 强制休息触发
        "stress_score_max": 85  # 综合压力分
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        super().__init__(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / "data" / "pressure_test.db"
        self.video_path = self.workspace / "data" / "test_videos"
        self.video_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._init_database()
        
        # WebSocket服务器状态
        self.observer_connections: Dict[str, websockets.WebSocketServerProtocol] = {}
        self.active_tests: Dict[str, Dict] = {}
    
    def _init_database(self):
        """初始化测试数据库"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 测试主表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tests (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT,
                    start_time TIMESTAMP,
                    end_time TIMESTAMP,
                    status TEXT,  # planned/running/paused/completed/aborted
                    subjects TEXT,  -- JSON数组
                    observers TEXT,  -- JSON数组
                    safety_officer TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            # 参与者表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS subjects (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT,
                    emergency_contact TEXT,
                    medical_notes TEXT,
                    consent_signed BOOLEAN,
                    consent_hash TEXT,  -- 电子签名哈希
                    consent_timestamp TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            
            # 观察记录表（PT-REQ-001: 多终端协同）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS observations (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    test_id TEXT,
                    observer_id TEXT,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    task_phase TEXT,
                    dimension_red TEXT,  -- JSON
                    dimension_blue TEXT,  -- JSON
                    dimension_green TEXT,  -- JSON
                    physiological_data TEXT,  -- JSON
                    video_timestamp REAL,
                    sync_status TEXT,  -- synced/pending/conflict
                    FOREIGN KEY (test_id) REFERENCES tests(id)
                )
            """)
            
            # 生理监控实时数据（PT-REQ-002）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS physiological_logs (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    test_id TEXT,
                    subject_id TEXT,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    heart_rate INTEGER,
                    sleep_minutes_last_24h INTEGER,
                    stress_indicator REAL,
                    alert_triggered BOOLEAN,
                    alert_type TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            # 知情同意书存储（PT-REQ-003）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS consent_forms (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    subject_id TEXT,
                    form_content TEXT,  -- 完整方案文本
                    signature_hash TEXT,  -- 电子签名哈希
                    signed_at TIMESTAMP,
                    ip_address TEXT,
                    user_agent TEXT,
                    blockchain_hash TEXT  -- 可选：上链存证
                )
            """)
            
            # 视频索引（PT-REQ-004）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS video_index (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    test_id TEXT,
                    camera_id TEXT,
                    file_path TEXT,
                    start_time TIMESTAMP,
                    duration_seconds REAL,
                    segment_markers TEXT,  -- JSON: 时间点->观察记录ID映射
                    audio_transcript TEXT  -- 转录文本（用于检索）
                )
            """)
            
            conn.commit()
    
    # === PT-REQ-003: 电子签署系统 ===
    
    @write_operation
    def generate_consent_form(self, subject: TestSubject, test_details: Dict) -> str:
        """
        生成知情同意书并创建签署链接
        包含时间戳和不可篡改哈希
        """
        form_content = f"""
        72小时压力测试知情同意书
        
        参与者: {subject.name}
        测试ID: {test_details.get('test_id')}
        时间: {datetime.now().isoformat()}
        
        风险告知：
        1. 本测试包含高强度心理压力场景
        2. 已配备医疗监护团队
        3. 可随时无条件退出
        
        同意声明：
        本人已阅读并理解上述风险，自愿参与测试。
        """
        
        # 生成表单ID和内容哈希
        form_id = f"CONSENT-{subject.subject_id}-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        content_hash = hashlib.sha256(form_content.encode()).hexdigest()
        
        # 存储表单
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO consent_forms 
                (id, subject_id, form_content, signed_at, blockchain_hash)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (form_id, subject.subject_id, form_content, None, content_hash))
            conn.commit()
        
        return form_id
    
    def sign_consent_electronically(self, form_id: str, signature_data: str, 
                                   ip_address: str, user_agent: str) -> Dict:
        """
        处理电子签名
        signature_data: 签名图像的Base64或哈希
        """
        # 计算签名哈希（防篡改）
        signature_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            f"{form_id}{signature_data}{datetime.now().isoformat()}".encode()
        ).hexdigest()
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 更新同意书
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE consent_forms 
                SET signature_hash = ?, signed_at = ?, ip_address = ?, user_agent = ?
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (signature_hash, datetime.now().isoformat(), ip_address, user_agent, form_id))
            
            # 更新参与者状态
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT subject_id FROM consent_forms WHERE id = ?", (form_id,))
            subject_id = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE subjects 
                SET consent_signed = 1, consent_timestamp = ?, consent_hash = ?
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), signature_hash, subject_id))
            
            conn.commit()
        
        return {
            "form_id": form_id,
            "signature_hash": signature_hash[:16] + "...",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "valid": True
        }
    
    # === PT-REQ-001: 多终端协同观察记录 ===
    
    async def start_websocket_server(self, host: str = "0.0.0.0", port: int = 8765):
        """
        启动WebSocket服务器供观察员实时录入
        支持离线缓存和冲突合并
        """
        async def handler(websocket, path):
            observer_id = None
            try:
                # 认证
                msg = await websocket.recv()
                auth_data = json.loads(msg)
                observer_id = auth_data.get("observer_id")
                test_id = auth_data.get("test_id")
                
                self.observer_connections[observer_id] = websocket
                await websocket.send(json.dumps({"status": "connected", "test_id": test_id}))
                
                # 接收观察记录
                async for message in websocket:
                    data = json.loads(message)
                    record = ObservationRecord(
                        record_id=f"OBS-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-{observer_id}",
                        test_id=test_id,
                        observer_id=observer_id,
                        timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
                        task_phase=data.get("phase"),
                        dimension_red=data.get("red", {}),
                        dimension_blue=data.get("blue", {}),
                        dimension_green=data.get("green", {}),
                        physiological_data=data.get("physio"),
                        video_timestamp=data.get("video_ts")
                    )
                    
                    # 保存到数据库
                    self._save_observation(record)
                    
                    # 检查安全红线
                    if record.physiological_data:
                        alert = self._check_safety_thresholds(
                            test_id, 
                            auth_data.get("subject_id"),
                            record.physiological_data
                        )
                        if alert:
                            await websocket.send(json.dumps({"alert": alert}))
                    
                    await websocket.send(json.dumps({"received": record.record_id}))
                    
            except websockets.exceptions.ConnectionClosed:
                pass
            finally:
                if observer_id and observer_id in self.observer_connections:
                    del self.observer_connections[observer_id]
        
        async with websockets.serve(handler, host, port):
            print(f"观察服务器启动于 ws://{host}:{port}")
            await asyncio.Future()  # 永久运行
    
    def _save_observation(self, record: ObservationRecord):
        """保存观察记录到SQLite"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO observations 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                record.record_id,
                record.test_id,
                record.observer_id,
                record.timestamp,
                record.task_phase,
                json.dumps(record.dimension_red),
                json.dumps(record.dimension_blue),
                json.dumps(record.dimension_green),
                json.dumps(record.physiological_data) if record.physiological_data else None,
                record.video_timestamp,
                "synced"
            ))
            conn.commit()
    
    # === PT-REQ-002: 生理安全监控 ===
    
    def _check_safety_thresholds(self, test_id: str, subject_id: str, 
                                  physio_data: Dict) -> Optional[Dict]:
        """
        实时监控生理指标，触及红线时告警
        """
        alerts = []
        
        hr = physio_data.get("heart_rate")
        if hr:
            if hr > self.SAFETY_THRESHOLDS["heart_rate_max"]:
                alerts.append({
                    "type": "CRITICAL",
                    "metric": "heart_rate",
                    "value": hr,
                    "threshold": self.SAFETY_THRESHOLDS["heart_rate_max"],
                    "action": "立即暂停测试，医疗介入"
                })
            elif hr < self.SAFETY_THRESHOLDS["heart_rate_min"]:
                alerts.append({
                    "type": "WARNING",
                    "metric": "heart_rate_low",
                    "value": hr,
                    "message": "心率过低，关注身体状态"
                })
        
        stress = physio_data.get("stress_indicator", 0)
        if stress > self.SAFETY_THRESHOLDS["stress_score_max"]:
            alerts.append({
                "type": "WARNING",
                "metric": "stress_overload",
                "value": stress,
                "action": "建议进入修复环节"
            })
        
        # 记录到数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO physiological_logs 
                (test_id, subject_id, timestamp, heart_rate, stress_indicator, alert_triggered, alert_type)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                test_id, subject_id, datetime.now().isoformat(),
                hr, stress, len(alerts) > 0, json.dumps(alerts) if alerts else None
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return alerts if alerts else None
    
    def trigger_multi_channel_alert(self, test_id: str, alert: Dict):
        """
        多通道告警（短信/微信/语音）
        实际实现需集成第三方服务，此处为框架
        """
        # 记录告警
        print(f"🚨 ALERT [{test_id}]: {alert}")
        
        # 飞书机器人告警（示例）
        feishu_webhook = self._get_config("feishu_webhook")
        if feishu_webhook:
            # requests.post(feishu_webhook, json={"msg_type": "text", "content": {"text": str(alert)}})
            pass
        
        return {"alert_sent": True, "channels": ["log", "websocket"]}
    
    # === PT-REQ-004: 视频自动索引 ===
    
    def index_video_file(self, test_id: str, camera_id: str, video_file: Path) -> Dict:
        """
        基于OpenCV的视频切片与索引
        按任务阶段自动切割
        """
        cap = cv2.VideoCapture(str(video_file))
        fps = cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FPS)
        total_frames = int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_COUNT))
        duration = total_frames / fps if fps > 0 else 0
        
        # 获取观察记录时间点作为切割标记
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT timestamp, video_timestamp, id 
                FROM observations 
                WHERE test_id = ? AND video_timestamp IS NOT NULL
                ORDER BY video_timestamp
            """, (test_id,))
            markers = [(row[1], row[2]) for row in cursor.fetchall()]  # (时间点, 记录ID)
        
        # 创建视频片段
        segments = []
        for i, (timestamp, record_id) in enumerate(markers):
            start_frame = int(timestamp * fps)
            end_frame = int(markers[i+1][0] * fps) if i+1 < len(markers) else total_frames
            
            segment_path = self.video_path / f"{test_id}_{camera_id}_seg{i}.mp4"
            
            # 使用OpenCV写入片段
            fourcc = cv2.VideoWriter_fourcc(*'mp4v')
            out = cv2.VideoWriter(str(segment_path), fourcc, fps, 
                                  (int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_WIDTH)),
                                   int(cap.get(cv2.CAP_PROP_FRAME_HEIGHT))))
            
            cap.set(cv2.CAP_PROP_POS_FRAMES, start_frame)
            for _ in range(start_frame, min(end_frame, total_frames)):
                ret, frame = cap.read()
                if not ret:
                    break
                out.write(frame)
            
            out.release()
            segments.append({
                "record_id": record_id,
                "file": str(segment_path),
                "start": timestamp,
                "duration": (end_frame - start_frame) / fps
            })
        
        cap.release()
        
        # 保存索引
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO video_index 
                (test_id, camera_id, file_path, start_time, duration_seconds, segment_markers)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                test_id, camera_id, str(video_file),
                datetime.now().isoformat(), duration,
                json.dumps({s["record_id"]: s["file"] for s in segments})
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return {"segments": len(segments), "indexed": True}
    
    def jump_to_video_by_observation(self, observation_id: str) -> Optional[str]:
        """
        点击观察记录跳转对应视频片段（PT-REQ-004核心功能）
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT vi.segment_markers 
                FROM observations obs
                JOIN video_index vi ON obs.test_id = vi.test_id
                WHERE obs.id = ?
            """, (observation_id,))
            
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            if row:
                markers = json.loads(row[0])
                return markers.get(observation_id)
        return None
    
    # === PT-REQ-005: 复盘与报告生成 ===
    
    def generate_assessment_report(self, test_id: str) -> Dict:
        """
        生成增强版评估报告：团队文化画像
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 获取所有观察数据
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT dimension_red, dimension_blue, dimension_green, timestamp
                FROM observations WHERE test_id = ?
            """, (test_id,))
            
            reds, blues, greens = [], [], []
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                reds.append(json.loads(row[0]))
                blues.append(json.loads(row[1]))
                greens.append(json.loads(row[2]))
        
        # 计算三维雷达图数据
        def avg_dimension(data_list: List[Dict], key: str) -> float:
            values = [d.get(key, 0) for d in data_list if d]
            return sum(values) / len(values) if values else 0
        
        radar_data = {
            "red": {
                "决断力": avg_dimension(reds, "decisiveness"),
                "抗压力": avg_dimension(reds, "stress_resistance"),
                "目标感": avg_dimension(reds, "goal_clarity")
            },
            "blue": {
                "质疑力": avg_dimension(blues, "skepticism"),
                "反思度": avg_dimension(blues, "reflection"),
                "逻辑性": avg_dimension(blues, "logic")
            },
            "green": {
                "共情力": avg_dimension(greens, "empathy"),
                "连接力": avg_dimension(greens, "connection"),
                "修复力": avg_dimension(greens, "repair")
            }
        }
        
        # 冲突模式聚类（简化版：基于方差）
        conflict_patterns = []
        red_variance = np.var([r.get("decisiveness", 0) for r in reds]) if reds else 0
        if red_variance > 50:
            conflict_patterns.append("高波动型：决策风格不稳定")
        
        # 修复能力路径建议
        repair_suggestions = []
        if radar_data["green"]["修复力"] < 60:
            repair_suggestions.append("建议增加冲突后修复训练模块")
        
        return {
            "test_id": test_id,
            "radar_chart": radar_data,
            "conflict_patterns": conflict_patterns,
            "repair_path": repair_suggestions,
            "raw_scores": {"red": reds, "blue": blues, "green": greens},
            "generated_at": datetime.now().isoformat()
        }
    
    def export_pdf_report(self, test_id: str, output_path: Path):
        """
        导出PDF报告（需要reportlab库）
        此处为占位实现
        """
        report_data = self.generate_assessment_report(test_id)
        # 实际实现使用FPDF或ReportLab生成PDF
        output_path.write_text(json.dumps(report_data, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        return output_path

# === CLI接口 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="72小时压力测试系统")
    parser.add_argument("--init-db", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--start-ws-server", action="store_true", help="启动WebSocket观察服务器")
    parser.add_argument("--index-video", nargs=3, metavar=("TEST_ID", "CAMERA_ID", "VIDEO_PATH"))
    parser.add_argument("--generate-report", type=str, metavar="TEST_ID")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    system = PressureTestSystem()
    
    if args.init_db:
        print("压力测试数据库已初始化")

        print("启动WebSocket服务器...")
        asyncio.run(system.start_websocket_server())
    
    if args.index_video:

        print(f"视频索引完成: {result}")
    
    if args.generate_report:
        report = system.generate_assessment_report(args.generate_report)
        print(json.dumps(report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))

🛡️ 批次三：监控、安全与异常处理层

3.1 系统监控与告警（MON-001~003, ROL-001~003）

文件: core/system_monitor.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
系统监控与熔断机制
需求覆盖: MON-001~003, ROL-001~003, SEC-001, TOK-001~004
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import psutil
import asyncio
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import git
import requests

from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent, ComponentMeta, write_operation

class SystemMonitor(BaseComponent):
    """
    统一监控面板 + 熔断机制
    """
    
    META = ComponentMeta(
        __version__="1.0.0",
        __dependencies__=["psutil", "sqlite3"],
        __category__="management",
        __author__="system",
        __baseline_required__=False  # 监控本身不触发基线检查，避免循环
    )
    
    # Token阈值配置（TOK-001, MON-002）
    TOKEN_LIMITS = {
        "daily_limit": 100000,
        "warning_threshold": 0.7,  # 70%告警
        "critical_threshold": 0.9  # 90%熔断
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        super().__init__(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "monitor.db"
        self.alert_log_path = self.workspace / "logs" / "alerts"
        self.alert_log_path.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        self._init_database()
    
    def _init_database(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 组件健康度表（MON-001）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS component_health (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    component_name TEXT,
                    last_run TIMESTAMP,
                    status TEXT,  # healthy/warning/critical
                    duration_ms REAL,
                    error_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0,
                    avg_memory_mb REAL
                )
            """)
            
            # Token消耗实时监控（MON-002）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS token_consumption (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    date TEXT UNIQUE,
                    consumed INTEGER,
                    limit INTEGER,
                    hourly_breakdown TEXT  -- JSON: 24小时分布
                )
            """)
            
            # 上游API稳定性（MON-003）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_health (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    api_name TEXT,
                    check_time TIMESTAMP,
                    available BOOLEAN,
                    response_ms INTEGER,
                    error_type TEXT,
                    consecutive_failures INTEGER
                )
            """)
            
            # 熔断记录（TOK-001）
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS circuit_breaker_logs (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    trigger_time TIMESTAMP,
                    trigger_reason TEXT,
                    action_taken TEXT,
                    auto_recovery_at TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            
            conn.commit()
    
    # === MON-001: 健康度监控面板 ===
    
    def check_component_health(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """检查所有注册组件健康状态"""
        from core.workspace_indexer import WorkspaceIndexer
        
        indexer = WorkspaceIndexer(str(self.workspace))
        registry_db = self.workspace / ".system" / "runtime.db"
        
        health_status = {}
        
        with sqlite3.connect(registry_db) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT name, last_run, run_count, avg_duration_ms 
                FROM component_registry
            """)
            
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                name, last_run, run_count, avg_duration = row
                
                # 计算状态
                if not last_run:
                    status = "never_run"
                else:
                    last_run_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(last_run)
                    hours_since = (datetime.now() - last_run_dt).total_seconds() / 3600
                    
                    if hours_since > 24:
                        status = "stale"
                    elif avg_duration and avg_duration > 30000:  # >30s
                        status = "slow"
                    else:
                        status = "healthy"
                
                health_status[name] = {
                    "status": status,
                    "last_run": last_run,
                    "run_count": run_count,
                    "avg_duration_ms": avg_duration
                }
                
                # 更新监控数据库
                with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn2:
                    conn2.execute("""
                        INSERT OR REPLACE INTO component_health 
                        (component_name, last_run, status, duration_ms)
                        VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
                    """, (name, last_run, status, avg_duration))
                    conn2.commit()
        
        return health_status
    
    def generate_dashboard(self) -> str:
        """生成监控面板Markdown"""
        health = self.check_component_health()
        
        lines = [
            "# 系统健康度监控面板",
            f"更新时间: {datetime.now().isoformat()}",
            "",
            "## 组件状态概览",
            "",
            "| 组件 | 状态 | 最后运行 | 平均耗时 |",
            "|------|------|----------|----------|"
        ]
        
        status_emoji = {
            "healthy": "🟢",
            "stale": "🟡",
            "slow": "🟠",
            "never_run": "🔴"
        }
        
        for comp, data in health.items():
            emoji = status_emoji.get(data["status"], "⚪")
            lines.append(f"| {comp} | {emoji} {data['status']} | {data['last_run'] or 'N/A'} | {data['avg_duration_ms']:.0f}ms |")
        
        # Token消耗
        lines.extend([
            "",
            "## Token预算消耗（今日）",
            ""
        ])
        
        token_status = self.check_token_budget()
        lines.append(f"- 已消耗: {token_status['consumed']} / {token_status['limit']}")
        lines.append(f"- 剩余: {token_status['remaining']}")
        lines.append(f"- 状态: {token_status['status']}")
        
        return "\n".join(lines)
    
    # === MON-002: Token熔断机制（TOK-001, TOK-002）===
    
    def check_token_budget(self) -> Dict:
        """检查Token预算，触发熔断"""
        today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT consumed, limit FROM token_consumption WHERE date = ?
            """, (today,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                # 初始化
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO token_consumption (date, consumed, limit, hourly_breakdown)
                    VALUES (?, 0, ?, ?)
                """, (today, self.TOKEN_LIMITS["daily_limit"], json.dumps({})))
                conn.commit()
                consumed, limit = 0, self.TOKEN_LIMITS["daily_limit"]
            else:
                consumed, limit = row
            
            remaining = limit - consumed
            usage_ratio = consumed / limit
            
            # 确定状态
            if usage_ratio > self.TOKEN_LIMITS["critical_threshold"]:
                status = "CRITICAL"
                action = "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE"
                self._trigger_circuit_breaker("token_limit_critical", "暂停所有非紧急任务")
            elif usage_ratio > self.TOKEN_LIMITS["warning_threshold"]:
                status = "WARNING"
                action = "THROTTLE_MODE"
            else:
                status = "HEALTHY"
                action = "NORMAL"
            
            return {
                "consumed": consumed,
                "limit": limit,
                "remaining": remaining,
                "usage_ratio": usage_ratio,
                "status": status,
                "action": action
            }
    
    def _trigger_circuit_breaker(self, reason: str, action: str):
        """触发熔断"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO circuit_breaker_logs (trigger_time, trigger_reason, action_taken)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?)
            """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), reason, action))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 写入标记文件（供其他组件检查）
        breaker_file = self.workspace / ".system" / "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE"
        breaker_file.write_text(json.dumps({
            "triggered_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "reason": reason,
            "action": action
        }))
        
        self._send_alert(f"🚨 熔断器激活: {reason} - {action}")
    
    def is_circuit_breaker_active(self) -> bool:
        """检查熔断器状态"""
        breaker_file = self.workspace / ".system" / "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE"
        if not breaker_file.exists():
            return False
        
        try:
            data = json.loads(breaker_file.read_text())
            triggered = datetime.fromisoformat(data["triggered_at"])
            # 熔断器1小时后自动尝试恢复
            if datetime.now() - triggered > timedelta(hours=1):
                breaker_file.unlink()
                return False
            return True
        except:
            return False
    
    # === MON-003: 上游API监控 ===
    
    def check_upstream_apis(self) -> Dict[str, Dict]:
        """检查Kimi/GitHub Models等上游API"""
        results = {}
        
        # 检查Kimi（通过简单请求）
        try:
            # 假设有一个健康检查端点或轻量API
            start = datetime.now()
            # 实际实现需替换为真实API检查
            response = requests.get("https://kimi-api.health", timeout=5)
            elapsed = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds() * 1000
            
            results["kimi"] = {
                "available": response.status_code == 200,
                "response_ms": elapsed,
                "error": None
            }
        except Exception as e:
            results["kimi"] = {
                "available": False,
                "response_ms": 0,
                "error": str(e)
            }
        
        # 记录到数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            for api_name, data in results.items():
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO api_health (api_name, check_time, available, response_ms, error_type)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (api_name, datetime.now().isoformat(), data["available"], 
                      data["response_ms"], data["error"]))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 检查连续失败次数
        for api_name in results.keys():
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                cursor = conn.execute("""
                    SELECT COUNT(*) FROM api_health 
                    WHERE api_name = ? AND available = 0 
                    AND check_time > ?
                """, (api_name, (datetime.now() - timedelta(hours=24)).isoformat()))
                failures = cursor.fetchone()[0]
                
                if failures > 3:  # 24小时内超过3次失败
                    self._send_alert(f"⚠️ API {api_name} 24小时内失败{failures}次，建议切换备用方案")
        
        return results
    
    # === ROL-001~003: 异常处理与回滚 ===
    
    def create_recovery_point(self, reason: str = "auto") -> str:
        """创建Git快照（复用基类方法）"""
        return self.create_git_snapshot(reason)
    
    def emergency_rollback(self, target: str = "last_stable") -> bool:
        """
        一键回滚到安全状态
        target: 'last_stable' 或特定stash引用
        """
        try:
            if target == "last_stable":
                # 找到最近一次标记为稳定的快照
                repo = git.Repo(self.workspace)
                # 查找带有"stable"标记的stash或tag
                return self.rollback_to_snapshot()
            else:
                return self.rollback_to_snapshot(target)
        except Exception as e:
            self._send_alert(f"❌ 回滚失败: {e}")
            return False
    
    def fail_safe_degradation(self, failed_component: str):
        """
        故障降级（ROL-002）
        当某组件失败时，确保不影响其他资产
        """
        # 记录失败
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE component_health 
                SET status = 'critical', error_count = error_count + 1
                WHERE component_name = ?
            """, (failed_component,))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 创建隔离标记
        isolation_file = self.workspace / ".system" / f"isolated_{failed_component}"
        isolation_file.write_text(datetime.now().isoformat())
        
        self._send_alert(f"⚠️ 组件 {failed_component} 已故障隔离，系统继续运行")
    
    # === 工具方法 ===
    
    def _send_alert(self, message: str):
        """发送告警（本地日志+飞书）"""
        # 本地日志
        alert_file = self.alert_log_path / f"alert_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}.log"
        with open(alert_file, "a") as f:
            f.write(f"{datetime.now().isoformat()} - {message}\n")
        
        print(f"ALERT: {message}")

# === CLI ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser()
    parser.add_argument("--dashboard", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--check-health", action="store_true")
    parser.add_argument("--rollback", type=str, help="回滚到指定快照")
    parser.add_argument("--isolate", type=str, help="隔离指定组件")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    monitor = SystemMonitor()
    
    if args.dashboard:
        print(monitor.generate_dashboard())
    
    if args.check_health:
        print(json.dumps(monitor.check_component_health(), indent=2))
    
    if args.rollback:
        success = monitor.emergency_rollback(args.rollback)
        print(f"回滚{'成功' if success else '失败'}")
    
    if args.isolate:
        monitor.fail_safe_degradation(args.isolate)

👥 批次四：外援协作与Token优化专项

4.1 外援接入控制器（COO-003）

文件: core/external_dev_controller.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
外援开发者接入控制器
需求覆盖: COO-003, STD-005, DOC-001
实现：最小权限清单、代码审查流程、知识沉淀检查
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import hashlib
import subprocess
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Set

from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent, ComponentMeta, write_operation

class ExternalDevController(BaseComponent):
    """
    外援开发者管理器
    确保外援产出符合系统约定
    """
    
    META = ComponentMeta(
        __version__="1.0.0",
        __dependencies__=["git", "sqlite3"],
        __category__="management",
        __author__="system"
    )
    
    # 外援权限白名单（最小权限原则）
    ALLOWED_OPERATIONS = {
        "read": ["*.py", "*.md", "docs/*", "data/*.db"],
        "write": ["systems/*.py", "apps/*.py", "tests/*.py"],
        "deny": ["core/base_component_v2.py", ".system/*", "memory/*"]
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        super().__init__(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "external_dev.db"
        self.review_queue_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "review_queue"
        self.review_queue_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self._init_database()
    
    def _init_database(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS external_devs (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    name TEXT,
                    email TEXT,
                    onboarded_at TIMESTAMP,
                    allowed_categories TEXT,  -- JSON: 可修改的类别
                    active BOOLEAN DEFAULT 1
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS code_submissions (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    dev_id TEXT,
                    file_path TEXT,
                    submitted_at TIMESTAMP,
                    status TEXT,  # pending/approved/rejected
                    review_comments TEXT,
                    compliance_check TEXT,  -- JSON: 元数据检查结果
                    knowledge_card_path TEXT  -- 知识卡片路径（DOC-001）
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    # === COO-003: 外援接入SOP ===
    
    def onboard_developer(self, dev_info: Dict) -> str:
        """
        外援入驻流程
        1. 生成最小权限配置
        2. 创建隔离工作区
        3. 注册到系统
        """
        dev_id = f"EXT-{dev_info['name']}-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d')}"
        
        # 创建隔离配置
        config = {
            "dev_id": dev_id,
            "allowed_write_paths": self.ALLOWED_OPERATIONS["write"],
            "deny_paths": self.ALLOWED_OPERATIONS["deny"],
            "require_meta_fields": ["__version__", "__dependencies__", "__category__", "__author__"],
            "require_review_before_merge": True
        }
        
        config_path = self.workspace / ".system" / f"dev_config_{dev_id}.json"
        config_path.write_text(json.dumps(config, indent=2))
        
        # 注册
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO external_devs VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                dev_id,
                dev_info["name"],
                dev_info["email"],
                datetime.now().isoformat(),
                json.dumps(["business", "intelligence"]),  # 默认允许业务类修改
                1
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return dev_id
    
    def validate_submission(self, dev_id: str, file_path: Path, content: str) -> Dict:
        """
        代码提交前的自动合规检查（蓝军预审）
        STD-002: 检查元数据
        STD-005: 检查Git提交信息
        """
        violations = []
        warnings = []
        
        # 检查1：是否在允许路径
        allowed = any(str(file_path).endswith(pattern.replace("*", "")) 
                     for pattern in self.ALLOWED_OPERATIONS["write"])
        if not allowed:
            violations.append(f"文件路径 {file_path} 不在允许写入范围内")
        
        # 检查2：Python文件的元数据（STD-002）
        if file_path.suffix == '.py':
            required_meta = ["__version__", "__dependencies__", "__category__", "__author__"]
            for meta in required_meta:
                if f"{meta} =" not in content and f"{meta}=" not in content:
                    violations.append(f"缺少必需元数据: {meta}")
        
        # 检查3：是否包含硬编码密钥（安全）
        suspicious_patterns = ["password", "secret", "token", "api_key"]
        for pattern in suspicious_patterns:
            if pattern in content.lower() and "=" in content:
                warnings.append(f"发现潜在敏感信息关键词: {pattern}")
        
        # 检查4：是否继承BaseComponent（业务类）
        if file_path.suffix == '.py' and "class" in content:
            if "BaseComponent" not in content and "systems/" in str(file_path):
                warnings.append("业务类建议继承BaseComponent以获得治理支持")
        
        return {
            "valid": len(violations) == 0,
            "violations": violations,
            "warnings": warnings,
            "auto_approved": len(violations) == 0 and len(warnings) <= 1
        }
    
    @write_operation
    def submit_for_review(self, dev_id: str, file_path: Path, content: str) -> str:
        """
        提交代码审查（四道门检查）
        """
        # 第一道门：自动合规检查
        validation = self.validate_submission(dev_id, file_path, content)
        
        if not validation["valid"]:
            return f"REJECTED_AUTO: {validation['violations']}"
        
        # 生成提交ID
        sub_id = f"SUB-{dev_id}-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}"
        
        # 保存到审查队列
        sub_path = self.review_queue_path / f"{sub_id}.json"
        sub_data = {
            "submission_id": sub_id,
            "dev_id": dev_id,
            "file_path": str(file_path),
            "content_hash": hashlib.sha256(content.encode()).hexdigest()[:16],
            "submitted_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "auto_validation": validation,
            "status": "pending_second_gate"  # 等待满意姐功能验收
        }
        sub_path.write_text(json.dumps(sub_data, indent=2))
        
        # 记录到数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO code_submissions 
                (id, dev_id, file_path, submitted_at, status, compliance_check)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (sub_id, dev_id, str(file_path), datetime.now().isoformat(),
                  "pending", json.dumps(validation)))
            conn.commit()
        
        return sub_id
    
    def complete_review(self, submission_id: str, reviewer: str, decision: str, comments: str):
        """
        完成人工审查（满意姐或蓝军）
        reviewer: 'manyige' (功能验收) / 'skeptor-7' (治理审计)
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE code_submissions 
                SET status = ?, review_comments = ?
                WHERE id = ?
            """, (decision, f"[{reviewer}] {comments}", submission_id))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 如果通过，触发知识卡片生成（DOC-001）
        if decision == "approved":
            self._generate_knowledge_card(submission_id)
    
    def _generate_knowledge_card(self, submission_id: str):
        """
        生成专家知识卡片（DOC-003）
        每个合并的PR必须附带知识沉淀
        """
        sub_path = self.review_queue_path / f"{submission_id}.json"
        if not sub_path.exists():
            return
        
        sub_data = json.loads(sub_path.read_text())
        
        card = {
            "submission_id": submission_id,
            "component": Path(sub_data["file_path"]).name,
            "change_summary": sub_data.get("change_summary", "待补充"),
            "architecture_impact": "待评估",
            "conventions_affected": [],
            "migration_guide": "",
            "rollback_procedure": f"使用 hash {sub_data['content_hash'][:8]} 回滚"
        }
        
        card_path = self.workspace / "docs" / "knowledge_cards" / f"{submission_id}.json"
        card_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        card_path.write_text(json.dumps(card, indent=2))
        
        # 更新数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE code_submissions SET knowledge_card_path = ? WHERE id = ?
            """, (str(card_path), submission_id))
            conn.commit()

# === 知识沉淀SOP（DOC-001: 约定即代码）===

class ConventionAsCode:
    """
    将运行级约定转换为机器可读配置
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.conventions_path = self.workspace / "config" / "conventions.yaml"
        self.conventions_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
    
    def generate_machine_config(self):
        """
        从Markdown协议文档生成YAML配置
        """
        config = {
            "silent_mode": {
                "pre_conditions": ["token_check", "git_clean", "task_clear"],
                "cache_duration_minutes": 30,
                "allowed_during_silence": ["read_only", "emergency_rollback"]
            },
            "deep_insight": {
                "mandatory_after_p0": True,
                "timeout_minutes": 10,
                "required_levels": ["L1", "L2", "L3", "L4", "L5"]
            },
            "honest_audit": {
                "min_concerns": 3,
                "forbidden_phrases": ["一切正常", "没有问题", "完美"],
                "triggers": ["p0_closure", "suspicious_quality"]
            },
            "token_optimization": {
                "context_compression_threshold": 30,  # 30轮对话后压缩
                "batch_similar_tasks": True,
                "cache_duration_hours": 24
            }
        }
        
        import yaml
        self.conventions_path.write_text(yaml.dump(config, allow_unicode=True))
        return str(self.conventions_path)

4.2 Token优化专项实现（TOK-001~004）

文件: core/token_optimizer.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
Token优化引擎
需求覆盖: TOK-001~004, EFF-004
"""

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import List, Dict, Optional, Any
import hashlib
import re

class TokenOptimizer:
    """
    Token效率优化工具集
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.cache_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "token_cache"
        self.cache_path.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "token_opt.db"
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS api_cache (
                    cache_key TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    content_hash TEXT,
                    result TEXT,
                    created_at TIMESTAMP,
                    expires_at TIMESTAMP,
                    access_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    # === TOK-001: 上下文压缩 ===
    
    def compress_context(self, conversation_history: List[Dict], threshold: int = 30) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        对话超过阈值时自动压缩为结构化摘要
        """
        if len(conversation_history) <= threshold:
            return conversation_history
        
        # 保留最近5轮完整对话
        recent = conversation_history[-5:]
        
        # 中间部分压缩为摘要
        middle = conversation_history[5:-5]
        
        summary = self._generate_summary(middle)
        
        compressed = [
            {"role": "system", "content": f"[前文摘要] {summary}"},
            *recent
        ]
        
        return compressed
    
    def _generate_summary(self, messages: List[Dict]) -> str:
        """生成中间对话的结构化摘要"""
        # 提取关键决策点和行动项
        key_points = []
        for msg in messages:
            content = msg.get("content", "")
            # 提取 TODO/决策/约定引用
            if any(k in content for k in ["决定", "约定", "TODO", "P0", "必须"]):
                key_points.append(content[:100])
        
        return f"共{len(messages)}轮对话，关键节点: " + "; ".join(key_points[:3])
    
    # === TOK-002: 批量处理 ===
    
    def batch_similar_tasks(self, tasks: List[Dict], template: str) -> Dict:
        """
        将多个相似任务合并为一次大prompt
        tasks: [{"file": "a.py", "instruction": "检查元数据"}, ...]
        """
        # 构建批量prompt
        batch_content = "请批量处理以下任务，按JSON格式返回结果:\n\n"
        for i, task in enumerate(tasks, 1):
            batch_content += f"[任务{i}] 文件: {task['file']}\n操作: {task['instruction']}\n\n"
        
        batch_content += "输出格式: {\"results\": [{\"task_id\": 1, \"status\": \"...\", \"details\": \"...\"}, ...]}"
        
        return {
            "consolidated_prompt": batch_content,
            "estimated_tokens": len(batch_content) // 4,  # 粗略估算
            "task_count": len(tasks),
            "individual_cost_estimate": sum(len(t["instruction"]) for t in tasks) // 4,
            "savings_ratio": 1 - (len(batch_content) / sum(len(t["instruction"]) for t in tasks))
        }
    
    # === TOK-003: 本地缓存最大化 ===
    
    def get_cached_or_fetch(self, cache_key: str, fetch_func, cache_hours: int = 24) -> Any:
        """
        带缓存的数据获取
        """
        cache_file = self.cache_path / f"{hashlib.md5(cache_key.encode()).hexdigest()}.json"
        
        # 检查缓存
        if cache_file.exists():
            try:
                data = json.loads(cache_file.read_text())
                created = datetime.fromisoformat(data["created_at"])
                if datetime.now() - created < timedelta(hours=cache_hours):
                    # 更新访问计数
                    data["access_count"] = data.get("access_count", 0) + 1
                    cache_file.write_text(json.dumps(data))
                    return data["result"]
            except:
                pass
        
        # 执行获取
        result = fetch_func()
        
        # 写入缓存
        cache_data = {
            "created_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "cache_key": cache_key[:50],  # 部分key用于调试
            "result": result,
            "access_count": 1
        }
        cache_file.write_text(json.dumps(cache_data, ensure_ascii=False))
        
        return result
    
    # === TOK-004: 静默模式轻量检查 ===
    
    def lightweight_silent_check(self, checklist_items: List[str]) -> Dict:
        """
        C1-C6轻量检查（仅检查存在性，不读全文）
        """
        results = {}
        
        for item in checklist_items:
            # 轻量检查：仅检查文件存在性或标记位
            if item == "git_clean":
                git_path = self.workspace / ".git"
                results[item] = not (self.workspace / ".git" / "index.lock").exists()
            
            elif item == "memory_synced":
                # 检查时间戳文件存在性，不读内容
                ts_file = self.workspace / "memory" / ".last_sync"
                results[item] = ts_file.exists()
            
            elif item == "critical_tasks_clear":
                # 检查标记文件存在性
                marker = self.workspace / ".system" / "P0_TASKS_PENDING"
                results[item] = not marker.exists()
            
            elif item.startswith("file_exists:"):
                filepath = self.workspace / item.split(":", 1)[1]
                results[item] = filepath.exists()
            
            else:
                results[item] = "unknown"
        
        all_passed = all(results.values()) if results else False
        
        return {
            "all_passed": all_passed,
            "checks": results,
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "check_type": "lightweight"
        }
    
    # === EFF-004: DOCX采样机制 ===
    
    def sample_large_document(self, file_path: Path, sample_ratio: float = 0.2) -> Dict:
        """
        大文档采样：先读前20%做快速洞察
        """
        if not file_path.exists():
            return {"error": "File not found"}
        
        content = file_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
        total_chars = len(content)
        sample_chars = int(total_chars * sample_ratio)
        
        # 取前sample_ratio部分 + 随机跳采样中间部分 + 结尾
        head = content[:sample_chars]
        
        # 简单的结构感知：尝试在段落边界截断
        last_period = head.rfind("。")
        if last_period > sample_chars * 0.8:
            head = head[:last_period+1]
        
        return {
            "total_chars": total_chars,
            "sample_chars": len(head),
            "sample_ratio": sample_ratio,
            "sample_content": head,
            "suggestion": "基于采样内容评估价值，确认高价值后再全量提取"
        }

📦 批次五：部署脚本与集成入口

5.1 主调度器升级（整合所有组件）

文件: daily_asset_runner_v2.py（升级版本）

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
日常资产调度器 V2.0 - 全系统治理入口
整合：索引、监控、Token优化、业务系统
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import argparse
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import subprocess

# 导入所有核心组件
from core.workspace_indexer import WorkspaceIndexer
from core.system_monitor import SystemMonitor
from core.token_optimizer import TokenOptimizer
from core.command_index_manager import CommandIndexManager, SilentModeProtocol
from core.external_dev_controller import ExternalDevController
from systems.emergence_matching_system import EmergenceMatchingSystem
from systems.pressure_test_72h_system import PressureTestSystem

class DailyAssetRunnerV2:
    """
    每日资产调度器 - 治理版本
    功能：分时段调度、依赖检测、健康检查
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.indexer = WorkspaceIndexer(workspace_root)
        self.monitor = SystemMonitor(workspace_root)
        self.token_opt = TokenOptimizer(workspace_root)
        
        # 调度配置
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "scheduler.db"
        self._init_scheduler()
    
    def _init_scheduler(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS schedule_log (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    asset_name TEXT,
                    scheduled_time TIMESTAMP,
                    actual_time TIMESTAMP,
                    status TEXT,
                    token_cost INTEGER,
                    output_summary TEXT
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def run_full_cycle(self, mode: str = "normal"):
        """
        执行完整调度周期
        mode: normal / silent (深夜低Token模式)
        """
        print(f"🚀 启动日常资产调度周期 - 模式: {mode} - {datetime.now().isoformat()}")
        
        # 1. 前置检查（Token预算、熔断器状态）
        if self.monitor.is_circuit_breaker_active():
            print("⚠️ 熔断器激活中，仅执行只读任务")
            return self._run_readonly_cycle()
        
        token_status = self.monitor.check_token_budget()
        if token_status["status"] == "CRITICAL":
            print("🚫 Token预算严重不足，调度终止")
            return
        
        # 2. 全局索引更新（非紧急，可跳过）
        if mode != "silent":
            print("📚 更新全局索引...")
            stats = self.indexer.full_indexing()
            print(f"   索引完成: {stats}")
        
        # 3. 健康度检查
        print("🏥 系统健康检查...")
        health = self.monitor.check_component_health()
        unhealthy = [k for k, v in health.items() if v["status"] != "healthy"]
        if unhealthy:
            print(f"   警告: 以下组件异常: {unhealthy}")
        
        # 4. 业务系统任务（按模式分配）
        if mode == "silent":
            # 深夜模式：只执行非紧急、低Token任务
            self._run_batch_tasks([
                ("token_cache_cleanup", self._cleanup_token_cache),
                ("git_garbage_collection", self._git_gc),
                ("index_optimization", self._optimize_index)
            ])
        else:
            # 正常模式：执行资产激活
            self._run_batch_tasks([
                ("emergence_matching_sync", self._sync_emergence_system),
                ("pressure_test_maintenance", self._maintain_pressure_system),
                ("monitoring_dashboard_update", self._update_dashboard)
            ])
        
        print(f"✅ 调度周期完成 - {datetime.now().isoformat()}")
    
    def _run_batch_tasks(self, tasks: list):
        """批量执行任务，带故障降级"""
        for name, task_func in tasks:
            try:
                print(f"   执行任务: {name}...")
                result = task_func()
                print(f"   完成: {name} - {result}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"   ❌ 任务失败 {name}: {e}")
                self.monitor.fail_safe_degradation(name)
    
    def _cleanup_token_cache(self):
        """清理过期Token缓存"""
        # 实现清理逻辑
        return "cleaned"
    
    def _git_gc(self):
        """Git仓库优化"""
        try:
            subprocess.run(["git", "gc"], cwd=self.workspace, check=True, capture_output=True)
            return "optimized"
        except:
            return "skipped"
    
    def _optimize_index(self):
        """数据库优化"""
        # 实现VACUUM等
        return "optimized"
    
    def _sync_emergence_system(self):
        """同步涌现匹配系统"""
        system = EmergenceMatchingSystem(str(self.workspace))
        # 执行数据同步等
        return "synced"
    
    def _maintain_pressure_system(self):
        """维护压力测试系统"""
        system = PressureTestSystem(str(self.workspace))
        return "maintained"
    
    def _update_dashboard(self):
        """更新监控面板"""
        dashboard = self.monitor.generate_dashboard()
        dashboard_path = self.workspace / "dashboard.md"
        dashboard_path.write_text(dashboard, encoding="utf-8")
        return f"updated: {dashboard_path}"

# === CLI入口 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="日常资产调度器V2")
    parser.add_argument("--mode", choices=["normal", "silent"], default="normal",
                       help="运行模式: normal(正常) / silent(深夜低Token模式)")
    parser.add_argument("--init-all", action="store_true", help="初始化所有子系统")
    
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    runner = DailyAssetRunnerV2()
    
    if args.init_all:
        print("🔧 初始化所有子系统...")
        # 初始化所有数据库和目录结构
        WorkspaceIndexer().full_indexing()
        CommandIndexManager()._sync_to_markdown()
        print("✅ 初始化完成")
    else:
        runner.run_full_cycle(mode=args.mode)
