🔧 批次六：调试指南与故障排除手册

6.1 系统诊断工具箱

文件: tools/system_diagnostic.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
系统诊断工具 - 一键检测所有组件健康状态
用于部署验证和日常排错
"""

import json
import sqlite3
import sys
import importlib
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Tuple

class SystemDiagnostic:
    """
    全系统诊断器 - 输出诊断报告
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root).resolve()
        self.report = {
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "workspace": str(self.workspace),
            "checks": {},
            "critical_issues": [],
            "warnings": [],
            "recommendations": []
        }
    
    def run_full_diagnostic(self) -> Dict:
        """执行完整诊断流程"""
        print("🔍 启动全系统诊断...")
        
        # 1. 基础设施检查
        self._check_python_environment()
        self._check_directory_structure()
        self._check_git_repository()
        
        # 2. 数据库健康检查
        self._check_sqlite_databases()
        
        # 3. 核心组件加载测试
        self._test_core_components()
        
        # 4. 元数据合规检查（STD-002）
        self._validate_metadata_compliance()
        
        # 5. 约定索引一致性（STD-001）
        self._check_convention_index()
        
        # 6. Token系统检查
        self._check_token_system()
        
        # 7. 生成修复脚本
        self._generate_fix_script()
        
        return self.report
    
    def _check_python_environment(self):
        """检查Python环境"""
        print("  [1/7] 检查Python环境...")
        checks = {
            "python_version": sys.version_info[:2],
            "required_modules": {}
        }
        
        required = ["sqlite3", "git", "websockets", "cv2", "psutil", "yaml", "requests"]
        for module in required:
            try:
                importlib.import_module(module)
                checks["required_modules"][module] = "✅"
            except ImportError:
                checks["required_modules"][module] = "❌ 未安装"
                self.report["warnings"].append(f"缺少模块: {module} (pip install {module})")
        
        self.report["checks"]["python_env"] = checks
    
    def _check_directory_structure(self):
        """检查目录结构完整性"""
        print("  [2/7] 检查目录结构...")
        required_dirs = [
            "core", "systems", "docs", "data", "memory", 
            "A-manyige/汇报", "A-manyige/对话",
            ".system", "logs", "apps"
        ]
        
        structure_status = {}
        for dir_path in required_dirs:
            full_path = self.workspace / dir_path
            exists = full_path.exists()
            structure_status[dir_path] = "✅" if exists else "❌"
            if not exists:
                self.report["critical_issues"].append(f"缺失必要目录: {dir_path}")
                # 自动修复建议
                self.report["recommendations"].append(f"mkdir -p {full_path}")
        
        self.report["checks"]["directory_structure"] = structure_status
    
    def _check_git_repository(self):
        """检查Git仓库状态"""
        print("  [3/7] 检查Git仓库...")
        try:
            import git
            repo = git.Repo(self.workspace)
            
            git_status = {
                "is_repo": True,
                "current_branch": repo.active_branch.name,
                "uncommitted_files": len(repo.index.diff(None)),
                "untracked_files": len(repo.untracked_files),
                "last_commit": repo.head.commit.hexsha[:8] if repo.head else "none"
            }
            
            if git_status["uncommitted_files"] > 0:
                self.report["warnings"].append(f"有 {git_status['uncommitted_files']} 个未提交变更")
            
            self.report["checks"]["git"] = git_status
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.report["critical_issues"].append(f"Git仓库异常: {e}")
            self.report["checks"]["git"] = {"is_repo": False, "error": str(e)}
    
    def _check_sqlite_databases(self):
        """检查所有SQLite数据库完整性"""
        print("  [4/7] 检查数据库...")
        db_files = [
            ".system/runtime.db",
            ".system/workspace_index.db", 
            ".system/command_index.db",
            ".system/monitor.db",
            "data/emergence_matching.db",
            "data/pressure_test.db",
            ".system/external_dev.db",
            ".system/token_opt.db"
        ]
        
        db_status = {}
        for db_file in db_files:
            db_path = self.workspace / db_file
            if not db_path.exists():
                db_status[db_file] = "⚠️ 不存在（将自动创建）"
                continue
            
            try:
                conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
                cursor = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM sqlite_master WHERE type='table'")
                tables = [row[0] for row in cursor.fetchall()]
                conn.close()
                
                db_status[db_file] = f"✅ ({len(tables)} 表)"
            except Exception as e:
                db_status[db_file] = f"❌ 损坏: {e}"
                self.report["critical_issues"].append(f"数据库损坏 {db_file}: {e}")
        
        self.report["checks"]["databases"] = db_status
    
    def _test_core_components(self):
        """测试核心组件加载"""
        print("  [5/7] 测试核心组件...")
        sys.path.insert(0, str(self.workspace))
        
        components = [
            ("core.base_component_v2", "BaseComponent"),
            ("core.workspace_indexer", "WorkspaceIndexer"),
            ("core.command_index_manager", "CommandIndexManager"),
            ("core.system_monitor", "SystemMonitor"),
            ("core.token_optimizer", "TokenOptimizer")
        ]
        
        component_status = {}
        for module_name, class_name in components:
            try:
                module = importlib.import_module(module_name)
                cls = getattr(module, class_name)
                # 尝试实例化（不连接实际数据库）
                component_status[f"{module_name}.{class_name}"] = "✅ 可加载"
            except Exception as e:
                component_status[f"{module_name}.{class_name}"] = f"❌ {str(e)[:50]}"
                self.report["critical_issues"].append(f"组件加载失败 {class_name}: {e}")
        
        self.report["checks"]["core_components"] = component_status
    
    def _validate_metadata_compliance(self):
        """验证元数据合规性（STD-002）"""
        print("  [6/7] 验证元数据合规...")
        systems_dir = self.workspace / "systems"
        
        if not systems_dir.exists():
            self.report["checks"]["metadata_compliance"] = "目录不存在"
            return
        
        violations = []
        required_meta = ["__version__", "__dependencies__", "__category__", "__author__"]
        
        for py_file in systems_dir.glob("*.py"):
            content = py_file.read_text(encoding="utf-8", errors="ignore")
            
            # 检查是否继承BaseComponent
            if "class" in content and "BaseComponent" not in content:
                violations.append(f"{py_file.name}: 未继承BaseComponent")
            
            # 检查元数据
            missing = [m for m in required_meta if f"{m} =" not in content and f"{m}=" not in content]
            if missing:
                violations.append(f"{py_file.name}: 缺少元数据 {missing}")
        
        self.report["checks"]["metadata_compliance"] = {
            "files_checked": len(list(systems_dir.glob("*.py"))),
            "violations": violations
        }
        
        if violations:
            self.report["warnings"].extend(violations[:5])  # 只显示前5个
    
    def _check_convention_index(self):
        """检查约定索引一致性"""
        print("  [7/7] 检查约定索引...")
        cmd_index_path = self.workspace / "docs" / "COMMAND_INDEX.md"
        
        if not cmd_index_path.exists():
            self.report["critical_issues"].append("COMMAND_INDEX.md 不存在")
            self.report["checks"]["convention_index"] = "❌ 缺失"
            return
        
        content = cmd_index_path.read_text(encoding="utf-8")
        
        # 检查关键约定是否存在
        required_conventions = ["SILENT_MODE_PROTOCOL", "DEEP_INSIGHT_PROTOCOL", "HONEST_AUDIT_PROTOCOL"]
        missing = [c for c in required_conventions if c not in content]
        
        self.report["checks"]["convention_index"] = {
            "exists": True,
            "registered_conventions": len(re.findall(r'\| CMD-\d+', content)),
            "missing_required": missing
        }
        
        if missing:
            self.report["warnings"].append(f"约定索引缺少: {missing}")
    
    def _generate_fix_script(self):
        """生成自动修复脚本"""
        fixes = []
        
        # 目录修复
        for rec in self.report["recommendations"]:
            if "mkdir" in rec:
                fixes.append(rec)
        
        # 数据库初始化修复
        for db_file, status in self.report["checks"].get("databases", {}).items():
            if "不存在" in str(status):
                fixes.append(f"# 初始化数据库 {db_file}: 运行对应模块的 __init__")
        
        self.report["auto_fix_commands"] = fixes
    
    def export_report(self):
        """导出诊断报告"""
        report_path = self.workspace / "logs" / f"diagnostic_{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d_%H%M%S')}.json"
        report_path.parent.mkdir(exist_ok=True)
        report_path.write_text(json.dumps(self.report, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
        
        # 同时输出可读版本
        md_report = self._format_markdown_report()
        md_path = report_path.with_suffix(".md")
        md_path.write_text(md_report, encoding="utf-8")
        
        return str(md_path)
    
    def _format_markdown_report(self) -> str:
        """格式化为Markdown报告"""
        lines = [
            "# 系统诊断报告",
            f"**时间**: {self.report['timestamp']}",
            f"**工作区**: {self.report['workspace']}",
            "",
            "## 执行摘要",
            f"- ✅ 通过检查: {len([c for c in self.report['checks'].values() if '❌' not in str(c)])}",
            f"- ⚠️ 警告: {len(self.report['warnings'])}",
            f"- ❌ 严重问题: {len(self.report['critical_issues'])}",
            "",
            "## 详细结果",
            ""
        ]
        
        for check_name, result in self.report["checks"].items():
            lines.append(f"### {check_name}")
            lines.append(f"```json")
            lines.append(json.dumps(result, indent=2, ensure_ascii=False))
            lines.append(f"```")
            lines.append("")
        
        if self.report["critical_issues"]:
            lines.extend([
                "## ❌ 严重问题（需立即修复）",
                ""
            ])
            for issue in self.report["critical_issues"]:
                lines.append(f"- [ ] {issue}")
            lines.append("")
        
        if self.report["warnings"]:
            lines.extend([
                "## ⚠️ 警告（建议优化）",
                ""
            ])
            for warning in self.report["warnings"]:
                lines.append(f"- [ ] {warning}")
            lines.append("")
        
        if self.report["auto_fix_commands"]:
            lines.extend([
                "## 🔧 建议修复命令",
                "```bash",
                *self.report["auto_fix_commands"],
                "```"
            ])
        
        return "\n".join(lines)

# === CLI ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    import argparse
    parser = argparse.ArgumentParser(description="系统诊断工具")
    parser.add_argument("--fix", action="store_true", help="尝试自动修复")
    args = parser.parse_args()
    
    diagnostic = SystemDiagnostic()
    report = diagnostic.run_full_diagnostic()
    
    # 输出摘要
    print(f"\n{'='*50}")
    print(f"诊断完成")
    print(f"严重问题: {len(report['critical_issues'])} | 警告: {len(report['warnings'])}")
    print(f"{'='*50}")
    
    # 导出报告
    report_path = diagnostic.export_report()
    print(f"📄 报告已保存: {report_path}")
    
    # 如果有严重问题，退出码非0
    if report['critical_issues']:
        sys.exit(1)

6.2 故障排除手册（Troubleshooting Guide）

文件: docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md（由诊断工具自动生成，但这里提供模板）

# 系统故障排除手册
> 生成时间: 2026-04-05
> 关联工具: `tools/system_diagnostic.py`

## 1. Token耗尽与熔断器触发

### 症状
- 系统返回 `Token budget exceeded`
- `.system/CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE` 文件存在
- 所有写入操作被拒绝

### 诊断步骤
```bash
# 检查当前Token消耗
python -c "from core.system_monitor import SystemMonitor; m=SystemMonitor(); print(m.check_token_budget())"

# 查看熔断日志
cat .system/CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE
cat logs/alerts/alert_$(date +%Y%m%d).log

解决方案

等待自动恢复: 熔断器1小时后自动解除

手动解除（仅限紧急情况）:

rm .system/CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE

切换到本地模式: 修改代码使用本地LLM而非API

2. 数据库锁定/损坏 (database is locked)

症状

SQLite错误: database is locked

或 file is not a database

诊断步骤

# 检查是否有僵尸进程占用
lsof .system/*.db

# 验证数据库完整性
sqlite3 .system/runtime.db "PRAGMA integrity_check;"

解决方案

终止占用进程:

# 找到并kill占用db的python进程
fuser -k .system/runtime.db

修复损坏数据库:

# 导出并重建
sqlite3 corrupt.db ".dump" > backup.sql
sqlite3 new.db < backup.sql
mv new.db corrupt.db

3. 组件循环依赖 (Circular Dependency)

症状

ImportError: cannot import name X

系统启动时无限递归

daily_asset_runner 卡在初始化

诊断步骤

# 生成依赖图
python -c "
from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent
import json
b = BaseComponent()
print(json.dumps(b.get_dependency_graph(), indent=2))
" > dependency_graph.json

解决方案

重构import顺序: 将基类import移至函数内部而非顶部

使用延迟加载:

# 替代直接import
def get_component():
    from systems.emergence_matching_system import EmergenceMatchingSystem
    return EmergenceMatchingSystem()

4. Git快照失败 (无法创建回滚点)

症状

create_git_snapshot 返回 error

提示 fatal: Unable to create '/workspace/.git/index.lock'

诊断

# 检查git状态
cd /workspace && git status

# 检查是否有未完成的合并
ls -la .git/*.lock

解决方案

清理Git锁:

rm -f .git/index.lock
rm -f .git/refs/heads/*.lock

手动创建快照:

git add .
git stash save "manual-snapshot-$(date +%Y%m%d-%H%M%S)"

5. 索引损坏 (搜索返回错误结果)

症状

workspace_indexer 返回不存在的文件

FTS5搜索报错

快速修复

# 重建索引
python -c "
from core.workspace_indexer import WorkspaceIndexer
idx = WorkspaceIndexer()
stats = idx.full_indexing()
print(f'重建完成: {stats}')
"

6. 伦理审查闸门误报 (合法数据被拦截)

症状

公开GitHub数据被标记为 non_public_source

候选人评估被错误拒绝

诊断

检查 logs/ethics_audit/ 下的日志:

grep "violation_found" logs/ethics_audit/ethics_$(date +%Y-%W).log

解决方案

手动标记合规（数据库操作）:

UPDATE candidates SET ethics_verified = 1 WHERE id = 'CAND-xxx';

调整检测规则: 修改 EmergenceMatchingSystem._log_ethics_event 的阈值

7. WebSocket观察服务器无响应 (PT-001)

症状

压力测试时观察员无法连接

手机端无法提交观察记录

诊断

# 检查端口占用
netstat -tulpn | grep 8765

# 测试WebSocket
python -c "
import asyncio
import websockets
async def test():
    async with websockets.connect('ws://localhost:8765') as ws:
        await ws.send('{\"observer_id\": \"test\"}')
        print(await ws.recv())
asyncio.run(test())
"

解决方案

重启服务器:

pkill -f "pressure_test_72h_system.py"
python systems/pressure_test_72h_system.py --start-ws-server &

检查防火墙:

# 确保8765端口开放
ufw allow 8765/tcp

8. 性能极慢 (单次操作>30秒)

可能原因

基线检查未命中缓存

数据库未索引

Token压缩未启用

诊断脚本

from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent
from datetime import datetime

class PerfTest(BaseComponent):
    def test_speed(self):
        start = datetime.now()
        self.baseline_check()  # 应该<1秒（缓存命中）
        duration = (datetime.now() - start).total_seconds()
        print(f"基线检查耗时: {duration}s {'(缓存未命中!)' if duration > 2 else '(正常)'}")

PerfTest().test_speed()

优化方案

确保缓存启用: 检查 .system/runtime.db 中的 baseline_cache 表

数据库VACUUM: 定期执行 sqlite3 db.db "VACUUM;"

启用采样模式: 大文档使用 TokenOptimizer.sample_large_document()

---

## 🧪 批次七：集成测试与验收方案

### 7.1 自动化测试套件

**文件**: `tests/integration_test_suite.py`

```python
#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
全系统集成测试套件
覆盖: E2E工作流、并发安全、故障恢复
"""

import unittest
import tempfile
import shutil
import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime

# 将workspace加入路径
import sys
sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).parent.parent))

from core.base_component_v2 import BaseComponent, ComponentMeta, write_operation
from core.workspace_indexer import WorkspaceIndexer
from core.command_index_manager import CommandIndexManager, SilentModeProtocol, DeepInsightProtocol
from core.system_monitor import SystemMonitor
from core.token_optimizer import TokenOptimizer
from systems.emergence_matching_system import EmergenceMatchingSystem, CandidateProfile
from systems.pressure_test_72h_system import PressureTestSystem, TestSubject

class TestInfrastructure(unittest.TestCase):
    """基础设施层测试"""
    
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.workspace = self.temp_dir / "test_workspace"
        self.workspace.mkdir()
        
    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir)
    
    def test_base_component_initialization(self):
        """测试基类初始化创建必要结构"""
        class TestComp(BaseComponent):
            META = ComponentMeta(
                __version__="1.0.0",
                __dependencies__=[],
                __category__="test",
                __author__="test"
            )
        
        comp = TestComp(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 验证数据库创建
        self.assertTrue((self.workspace / ".system" / "runtime.db").exists())
        
        # 验证组件注册
        with sqlite3.connect(self.workspace / ".system" / "runtime.db") as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM component_registry WHERE name = 'TestComp'")
            self.assertIsNotNone(cursor.fetchone())
    
    def test_baseline_caching(self):
        """测试基线检查缓存机制"""
        class TestComp(BaseComponent):
            META = ComponentMeta(__version__="1.0.0", __dependencies__=[], __category__="test", __author__="test")
            
            def _run_full_baseline_check(self):
                return {"status": "healthy", "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat()}
        
        comp = TestComp(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 第一次检查（应执行完整检查）
        result1 = comp.baseline_check()
        self.assertEqual(result1["status"], "healthy")
        
        # 第二次检查（应命中缓存）
        result2 = comp.baseline_check()
        self.assertEqual(result2.get("level"), "L0")  # 缓存标记

class TestGovernance(unittest.TestCase):
    """治理层测试"""
    
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.workspace = self.temp_dir / "test_workspace"
        self.workspace.mkdir()
        (self.workspace / "docs").mkdir()
        
    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir)
    
    def test_command_index_registration(self):
        """测试约定注册"""
        manager = CommandIndexManager(str(self.workspace))
        
        from core.command_index_manager import CommandProtocol
        
        protocol = CommandProtocol(
            cmd_id="TEST-001",
            name="TEST_PROTOCOL",
            version="V1.0",
            file_path="docs/TEST.md",
            description="测试协议",
            triggers=["manual"],
            output_format="JSON",
            related_cmds=[],
            priority="P0"
        )
        
        manager.register_command(protocol)
        
        # 验证Markdown索引生成
        index_path = self.workspace / "docs" / "COMMAND_INDEX.md"
        self.assertTrue(index_path.exists())
        content = index_path.read_text()
        self.assertIn("TEST_PROTOCOL", content)
    
    def test_silent_mode_protocol(self):
        """测试静默模式前置检查"""
        context = {
            "token_remaining": 10000,
            "has_p0_tasks": False,
            "git_clean": True,
            "last_sync_within_1h": True,
            "api_healthy": True,
            "emergency_config_exists": True
        }
        
        result = SilentModeProtocol.generate_pre_hibernation_checklist(context)
        
        self.assertTrue(result["can_enter_silent_mode"])
        self.assertEqual(len(result["checklist"]), 6)  # C1-C6
    
    def test_deep_insight_levels(self):
        """测试L1-L5洞察生成"""
        raw_data = {
            "candidate": "张三",
            "score": 85,
            "dimensions": {"red": 90, "blue": 80}
        }
        
        report = DeepInsightProtocol.generate_insight_report(raw_data, "test_context")
        
        self.assertIn("L1", report["levels"])
        self.assertIn("L5", report["levels"])
        self.assertTrue(len(report["levels"]["L5"]["recommendations"]) > 0)

class TestBusinessSystems(unittest.TestCase):
    """业务系统测试"""
    
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.workspace = self.temp_dir / "test_workspace"
        self.workspace.mkdir()
        
    def tearDown(self):
        shutil.rmtree(self.temp_dir)
    
    def test_emergence_matching_workflow(self):
        """测试涌现匹配完整流程"""
        system = EmergenceMatchingSystem(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 创建候选人
        candidate = CandidateProfile(
            candidate_id="TEST-001",
            name="测试候选人",
            email="test@example.com",
            github_username="octocat"  # 使用存在的用户测试
        )
        
        # 保存到数据库（模拟）
        with sqlite3.connect(system.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO candidates (id, name, email, created_at, assessment_status)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (candidate.candidate_id, candidate.name, candidate.email, datetime.now().isoformat(), "pending"))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 验证数据库记录
        with sqlite3.connect(system.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT name FROM candidates WHERE id = ?", (candidate.candidate_id,))
            self.assertEqual(cursor.fetchone()[0], "测试候选人")
    
    def test_pressure_test_safety_threshold(self):
        """测试压力测试安全红线"""
        system = PressureTestSystem(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 测试心率超限
        physio_data = {"heart_rate": 190, "stress_indicator": 50}
        alerts = system._check_safety_thresholds("TEST-001", "SUB-001", physio_data)
        
        self.assertIsNotNone(alerts)
        self.assertTrue(any(a["type"] == "CRITICAL" for a in alerts))
        
        # 验证数据库记录
        with sqlite3.connect(system.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("SELECT alert_triggered FROM physiological_logs LIMIT 1")
            self.assertTrue(cursor.fetchone()[0])

class TestTokenOptimization(unittest.TestCase):
    """Token优化测试"""
    
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.workspace = self.temp_dir / "test_workspace"
        self.workspace.mkdir()
        
    def test_context_compression(self):
        """测试上下文压缩"""
        optimizer = TokenOptimizer(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 生成35轮对话历史
        history = [
            {"role": "user", "content": f"消息{i}"} 
            for i in range(35)
        ]
        
        compressed = optimizer.compress_context(history, threshold=30)
        
        # 应压缩为摘要+最近5轮
        self.assertLess(len(compressed), len(history))
        self.assertTrue(any("前文摘要" in str(msg.get("content", "")) for msg in compressed))
    
    def test_cache_mechanism(self):
        """测试API结果缓存"""
        optimizer = TokenOptimizer(str(self.workspace))
        
        call_count = [0]
        
        def mock_fetch():
            call_count[0] += 1
            return {"data": "test_result"}
        
        # 第一次调用
        result1 = optimizer.get_cached_or_fetch("test_key", mock_fetch, cache_hours=1)
        # 第二次调用（应命中缓存）
        result2 = optimizer.get_cached_or_fetch("test_key", mock_fetch, cache_hours=1)
        
        self.assertEqual(result1, result2)
        self.assertEqual(call_count[0], 1)  # 只执行一次

class TestFailureRecovery(unittest.TestCase):
    """故障恢复测试"""
    
    def setUp(self):
        self.temp_dir = Path(tempfile.mkdtemp())
        self.workspace = self.temp_dir / "test_workspace"
        self.workspace.mkdir()
        
        # 初始化git
        import subprocess
        subprocess.run(["git", "init"], cwd=self.workspace, check=True, capture_output=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.email", "test@test.com"], cwd=self.workspace, check=True)
        subprocess.run(["git", "config", "user.name", "Test"], cwd=self.workspace, check=True)
        
    def test_git_snapshot_and_rollback(self):
        """测试Git快照与回滚"""
        system = SystemMonitor(str(self.workspace))
        
        # 创建测试文件
        test_file = self.workspace / "test_rollback.txt"
        test_file.write_text("version1")
        
        # 创建快照
        snapshot = system.create_git_snapshot(reason="test")
        self.assertNotEqual(snapshot, "unknown")
        
        # 修改文件
        test_file.write_text("version2")
        
        # 回滚
        success = system.rollback_to_snapshot()
        self.assertTrue(success)
        
        # 验证回滚成功
        self.assertEqual(test_file.read_text(), "version1")

def run_all_tests():
    """运行全部测试并生成报告"""
    loader = unittest.TestLoader()
    suite = unittest.TestSuite()
    
    # 添加所有测试类
    suite.addTests(loader.loadTestsFromTestCase(TestInfrastructure))
    suite.addTests(loader.loadTestsFromTestCase(TestGovernance))
    suite.addTests(loader.loadTestsFromTestCase(TestBusinessSystems))
    suite.addTests(loader.loadTestsFromTestCase(TestTokenOptimization))
    suite.addTests(loader.loadTestsFromTestCase(TestFailureRecovery))
    
    runner = unittest.TextTestRunner(verbosity=2)
    result = runner.run(suite)
    
    # 生成JSON报告
    report = {
        "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
        "tests_run": result.testsRun,
        "failures": len(result.failures),
        "errors": len(result.errors),
        "success": result.wasSuccessful()
    }
    
    print(f"\n{'='*60}")
    print(f"测试报告: {report['tests_run']} 个测试")
    print(f"失败: {report['failures']} | 错误: {report['errors']}")
    print(f"状态: {'✅ 全部通过' if report['success'] else '❌ 存在失败'}")
    print(f"{'='*60}")
    
    return report

if __name__ == "__main__":
    run_all_tests()

#!/bin/bash
# 蓝军系统一键部署脚本
# 用法: ./quick_start.sh /path/to/workspace

set -e  # 遇到错误立即退出

WORKSPACE=${1:-"/opt/blue-team-workspace"}
echo "🚀 开始部署蓝军系统到 $WORKSPACE"

# 1. 创建目录结构

# 2. 检查Python环境
echo "🐍 检查Python环境..."
python3 -c "import sys; assert sys.version_info >= (3, 12), '需要Python 3.12+'"

# 3. 安装依赖
echo "📦 安装Python依赖..."
pip install -q GitPython psutil websockets opencv-python requests pyyaml rich pydantic pandas

# 4. 假设代码文件已通过git clone或scp放入$WORKSPACE

    echo "❌ 错误: 核心文件未找到，请先将代码文件放入 $WORKSPACE"
    exit 1

# 5. 初始化系统
echo "🔧 初始化系统..."
cd $WORKSPACE
python3 daily_asset_runner_v2.py --init-all

# 9. 配置Cron（可选）

if [[ $REPLY =~ ^[Yy]$ ]]; then
    (crontab -l 2>/dev/null; echo "0 3 * * * cd $WORKSPACE && python3 daily_asset_runner_v2.py --mode silent >> logs/cron.log 2>&1") | crontab -
    (crontab -l 2>/dev/null; echo "0 9 * * * cd $WORKSPACE && python3 daily_asset_runner_v2.py --mode normal >> logs/cron.log 2>&1") | crontab -
    echo "✅ Cron任务已配置"
fi

echo ""
echo "🎉 部署完成!"
echo "📊 监控面板: $WORKSPACE/dashboard.md"
echo "📖 故障排除: $WORKSPACE/docs/TROUBLESHOOTING.md"
echo ""
echo "常用命令:"
echo "  手动调度: python3 daily_asset_runner_v2.py --mode normal"
echo "  系统诊断: python3 tools/system_diagnostic.py"
echo "  运行测试: python3 tests/integration_test_suite.py"

🧠 第一维度：元治理与认知架构（Meta-Governance）

1.1 核心洞察：当前系统的”治理悖论”

问题识别： - 你们正在用重治理解决规模问题，但治理本身成为新的熵增源 - 43个脚本的治理成本可能已经超过其产出价值（隐性技术债务） - “蓝军审计”如果过度，会异化为流程暴力，抑制满意姐的创造性产出

突破方案：三层认知外包架构

┌─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┐
│  Layer 3: 元治理层 (Meta-Governance)                        │
│  治理规则的自我修正 + 认知负载均衡                             │
│  组件: GovernanceDAO, CognitiveLoadBalancer                 │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Layer 2: 智能体层 (Agent Layer) ← 当前方案主要集中于此      │
│  业务逻辑 + 自动化运行                                        │
│  组件: EmergenceMatching, PressureTest, Indexer             │
├─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┤
│  Layer 1: 原始认知层 (Raw Cognition)                         │
│  Egbertie/满意姐的不可编码直觉 + 蓝军的质疑本能               │
│  保护区: TacitKnowledgeVault (明确定义AI不可触碰的认知域)      │
└─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────┘

关键创新：认知负载均衡器（Cognitive Load Balancer）

文件: meta/cognitive_load_balancer.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
认知负载均衡器 - 防止"治理过载"导致的人类认知退化
原理：基于认知负荷理论（Cognitive Load Theory），动态调节AI辅助强度
"""

import json
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
import random

@dataclass
class CognitiveState:
    """Egbertie的认知状态快照"""
    timestamp: str
    decision_fatigue_score: float  # 0-100, 基于连续决策次数和复杂度
    context_switch_count: int      # 过去1小时上下文切换次数
    error_rate_trend: float        # 近期错误率趋势
    willpower_reserve: float       # 意志力储备（基于生物节律估算）
    flow_state_depth: float        # 心流深度（基于连贯输出长度估算）

class CognitiveLoadBalancer:
    """
    认知负载均衡器：防止AI辅助导致的"认知肌肉萎缩"
    
    核心机制：
    1. 认知健身房模式：故意撤回AI辅助，强制人类思考
    2. 决策疲劳检测：连续复杂决策后，强制休息或降级任务
    3. 上下文切换税：量化并减少不必要的注意力跳转
    """
    
    # 认知保护阈值（基于认知心理学研究）
    THRESHOLDS = {
        "decision_fatigue_critical": 75,    # 超过此值，禁止复杂决策
        "context_switch_max": 10,            # 每小时最大切换次数
        "willpower_minimum": 20,             # 意志力红线
        "flow_protection_minutes": 25        # 进入心流后25分钟内不打断
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "cognitive_state.db"
        self._init_db()
        
        # 当前干预模式
        self.intervention_mode = "adaptive"  # adaptive/restrictive/gym
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cognitive_log (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    fatigue_score REAL,
                    switch_count INTEGER,
                    intervention_taken TEXT,
                    task_type TEXT,
                    ai_assistance_level INTEGER  -- 0-100%
                )
            """)
            
            # 认知健身房计划表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS cognitive_gym_schedule (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    scheduled_time TIMESTAMP,
                    exercise_type TEXT,  -- 'solo_decision', 'manual_code', 'no_ai_writing'
                    duration_minutes INTEGER,
                    completed BOOLEAN
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def assess_current_state(self, recent_activity: List[Dict]) -> CognitiveState:
        """
        评估当前认知状态
        输入：最近1小时的活动日志（来自Kimi对话历史或手动输入）
        """
        # 计算决策疲劳（基于任务复杂度标记）
        complex_decisions = sum(1 for act in recent_activity 
                               if act.get("complexity") in ["architectural", "ethical", "strategic"])
        fatigue = min(complex_decisions * 15, 100)  # 每个复杂决策+15分疲劳
        
        # 计算上下文切换
        contexts = [act.get("context_id") for act in recent_activity]
        switches = sum(1 for i in range(1, len(contexts)) if contexts[i] != contexts[i-1])
        
        # 意志力储备（基于时间：下午2-4点最低）
        hour = datetime.now().hour
        willpower = 100 - abs(hour - 14) * 10  # 下午2点最低谷
        
        # 心流深度（基于连续专注时间）
        continuous_focus = self._calculate_flow_depth(recent_activity)
        
        state = CognitiveState(
            timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
            decision_fatigue_score=fatigue,
            context_switch_count=switches,
            error_rate_trend=0.0,  # 需外部输入
            willpower_reserve=max(willpower, 10),
            flow_state_depth=continuous_focus
        )
        
        return state
    
    def determine_ai_assistance_level(self, task_type: str, state: CognitiveState) -> Dict:
        """
        决定当前任务应给予多少AI辅助（0-100%）
        
        突破点：不是"越多越好"，而是"恰到好处的摩擦"
        """
        base_assistance = {
            "routine_coding": 90,
            "architecture_design": 60,
            "creative_writing": 40,
            "ethical_judgment": 20,  # 伦理判断必须保留人类主导
            "emergency_response": 95
        }.get(task_type, 70)
        
        # 根据认知状态调整
        adjustments = 0
        
        # 决策疲劳高：增加辅助，但标记为"危险依赖"
        if state.decision_fatigue_score > self.THRESHOLDS["decision_fatigue_critical"]:
            adjustments += 20
            flag = "high_fatigue_dependency_warning"
        
        # 上下文切换过多：强制进入"单线程模式"（减少AI建议数量）
        elif state.context_switch_count > self.THRESHOLDS["context_switch_max"]:
            adjustments -= 30
            flag = "focus_protection_mode"
        
        # 心流状态中：最小干预，仅错误检查
        elif state.flow_state_depth > 0.7:
            adjustments -= 40
            flag = "flow_state_protection"
        
        # 意志力低：启用"认知健身房"（故意减少辅助）
        elif state.willpower_reserve < self.THRESHOLDS["willpower_minimum"]:
            if self._should_trigger_cognitive_gym():
                base_assistance = 30  # 强制减少辅助
                flag = "cognitive_gym_enforced"
            else:
                adjustments += 10
                flag = "low_willpower_support"
        
        final_level = max(10, min(100, base_assistance + adjustments))
        
        return {
            "assistance_level": final_level,
            "reasoning": flag,
            "recommendations": self._generate_recommendations(state, final_level),
            "human_must_lead": final_level < 50
        }
    
    def _calculate_flow_depth(self, activities: List[Dict]) -> float:
        """计算心流深度（0-1）"""
        if not activities:
            return 0.0
        
        # 检查连续时间窗口
        continuous_blocks = []
        current_block = 0
        
        for i, act in enumerate(activities):
            if i == 0:
                current_block = 1
            else:
                # 如果间隔<5分钟，认为是连续的
                prev_time = datetime.fromisoformat(activities[i-1]["timestamp"])
                curr_time = datetime.fromisoformat(act["timestamp"])
                if (curr_time - prev_time).seconds < 300:
                    current_block += 1
                else:
                    continuous_blocks.append(current_block)
                    current_block = 1
        
        continuous_blocks.append(current_block)
        max_block = max(continuous_blocks)
        
        # 25分钟以上的连续专注视为深度心流（Pomodoro标准）
        return min(max_block / 5, 1.0)  # 5个5分钟块=25分钟
    
    def _should_trigger_cognitive_gym(self) -> bool:
        """判断是否应触发认知健身房（基于间隔，避免连续触发）"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT MAX(timestamp) FROM cognitive_gym_schedule 
                WHERE completed = 1
            """)
            last_gym = cursor.fetchone()[0]
            
            if not last_gym:
                return True
            
            last_time = datetime.fromisoformat(last_gym)
            # 至少间隔4小时
            return (datetime.now() - last_time) > timedelta(hours=4)
    
    def schedule_cognitive_gym(self):
        """
        安排认知健身训练
        模式：故意创造"必要的困难"（Desirable Difficulties）
        """
        exercises = [
            {
                "type": "solo_architecture_decision",
                "description": "独立设计一个组件架构，不使用AI建议，手写后让AI审查",
                "duration": 30,
                "cognitive_benefit": "防止架构直觉退化"
            },
            {
                "type": "manual_code_review",
                "description": "人工阅读50行代码找出bug，再与AI结果对比",
                "duration": 20,
                "cognitive_benefit": "保持代码敏感度"
            },
            {
                "type": "tacit_articulation",
                "description": "将模糊的直觉转化为明确的规则（显性化练习）",
                "duration": 25,
                "cognitive_benefit": "提升元认知能力"
            },
            {
                "type": "constraint_creativity",
                "description": "在严格限制下（如无循环、无递归）解决一个问题",
                "duration": 20,
                "cognitive_benefit": "增强创造性压力耐受"
            }
        ]
        
        # 随机选择一个安排在今天
        selected = random.choice(exercises)
        schedule_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(hours=2)  # 2小时后
        
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO cognitive_gym_schedule 
                (scheduled_time, exercise_type, duration_minutes, completed)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (schedule_time.isoformat(), selected["type"], selected["duration"], False))
            conn.commit()
        
        return {
            "scheduled_at": schedule_time.isoformat(),
            "exercise": selected,
            "notification": "🧠 认知健身房已预约"
        }
    
    def _generate_recommendations(self, state: CognitiveState, assistance_level: int) -> List[str]:
        """生成个性化建议"""
        recs = []
        
        if state.decision_fatigue_score > 70:
            recs.append("⚠️ 决策疲劳高：推迟非紧急决策20分钟")
        
        if state.context_switch_count > 8:
            recs.append("🎯 上下文切换过多：建议进行25分钟单任务冲刺（Pomodoro）")
        
        if assistance_level > 80:
            recs.append("🤖 高AI依赖模式：注意检查AI幻觉，关键决策需人工确认")
        elif assistance_level < 40:
            recs.append("💪 认知健身房模式：利用此机会锻炼独立解决能力")
        
        if state.flow_state_depth > 0.8:
            recs.append("🌊 深度心流保护中：关闭所有通知，继续当前任务")
        
        return recs
    
    def record_interaction(self, task_type: str, ai_level: int, human_satisfaction: int):
        """记录交互反馈，用于后续优化"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO cognitive_log 
                (timestamp, fatigue_score, intervention_taken, task_type, ai_assistance_level)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), 0, "none", task_type, ai_level))
            conn.commit()

# === 使用示例 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    balancer = CognitiveLoadBalancer()
    
    # 模拟近期活动
    mock_activities = [
        {"timestamp": (datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=5)).isoformat(), 
         "complexity": "architectural", "context_id": "emergence_system"},
        {"timestamp": (datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=3)).isoformat(), 
         "complexity": "routine", "context_id": "emergence_system"},
        {"timestamp": (datetime.now() - timedelta(minutes=1)).isoformat(), 
         "complexity": "strategic", "context_id": "business_model"},
    ]
    
    state = balancer.assess_current_state(mock_activities)
    decision = balancer.determine_ai_assistance_level("architecture_design", state)
    
    print(f"认知状态: 疲劳{state.decision_fatigue_score}%, 切换{state.context_switch_count}次")
    print(f"建议AI辅助级别: {decision['assistance_level']}%")
    print(f"理由: {decision['reasoning']}")
    print("建议:")
    for rec in decision['recommendations']:
        print(f"  - {rec}")

1.2 元治理DAO：治理规则的链式演化

核心思想：治理规则本身需要被治理。当前COMMAND_INDEX.md是中心化的，应改为类区块链的不可篡改治理日志。

文件: meta/governance_dao.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
GovernanceDAO - 治理规则的链式演化
解决：规则是谁改的？为什么改？蓝军是否同意？满意姐是否知情？
技术：使用Merkle树确保治理历史不可篡改（无需真区块链，仅用哈希链）
"""

import json
import hashlib
import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass, asdict

@dataclass
class GovernanceProposal:
    """治理规则变更提案"""
    proposal_id: str
    timestamp: str
    proposer: str  # manyige / skeptor-7 / egbertie / external_dev
    rule_id: str   # 如 CMD-001
    change_type: str  # create / amend / deprecate
    from_version: str
    to_version: str
    rationale: str  # 变更理由
    impact_assessment: Dict  # 影响评估
    blue_team_review: Optional[Dict] = None  # 蓝军审计结果
    stakeholder_votes: Dict = None  # 投票记录

class GovernanceDAO:
    """
    治理DAO：确保规则变更的可追溯性和共识
    
    工作流程：
    1. 满意姐发起提案（提高产出效率）
    2. 蓝军自动审计（检查约定一致性）
    3. Egbertie最终裁决（或双负责人达成共识）
    4. 上链（写入Merkle树）
    5. 自动更新所有关联系统的配置
    """
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "governance_chain.db"
        self.chain_file = self.workspace / ".system" / "governance_chain.jsonl"
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            # 治理提案表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS proposals (
                    id TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    proposer TEXT,
                    rule_id TEXT,
                    change_type TEXT,
                    status TEXT,  # pending / blue_reviewed / approved / rejected / enacted
                    merkle_hash TEXT,
                    parent_hash TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            # 投票记录表
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS votes (
                    proposal_id TEXT,
                    voter TEXT,
                    vote TEXT,  # approve / reject / abstain
                    reason TEXT,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def propose_change(self, proposal: GovernanceProposal) -> Dict:
        """
        提交治理变更提案
        触发蓝军自动审计流程
        """
        # 生成唯一ID
        prop_id = f"PROP-{datetime.now().strftime('%Y%m%d%H%M%S')}-{proposal.proposer[:3]}"
        
        # 获取前一个区块的哈希（构建链）
        parent_hash = self._get_last_block_hash()
        
        # 计算当前提案的Merkle哈希
        proposal_data = json.dumps(asdict(proposal), sort_keys=True)
        current_hash = hashlib.sha256(
            f"{parent_hash}{proposal_data}".encode()
        ).hexdigest()[:16]
        
        # 存入数据库（待审计状态）
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO proposals 
                (id, timestamp, proposer, rule_id, change_type, status, merkle_hash, parent_hash)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (
                prop_id, proposal.timestamp, proposal.proposer, 
                proposal.rule_id, proposal.change_type, "pending",
                current_hash, parent_hash
            ))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 触发蓝军自动审计（模拟）
        blue_review = self._trigger_blue_review(proposal)
        
        return {
            "proposal_id": prop_id,
            "status": "pending_blue_review",
            "merkle_hash": current_hash,
            "parent_hash": parent_hash,
            "blue_review_scheduled": True,
            "estimated_review_time": "10_minutes"
        }
    
    def _trigger_blue_review(self, proposal: GovernanceProposal) -> Dict:
        """
        蓝军自动审计治理提案
        检查：是否与现有约定冲突？是否引入循环依赖？Token效率影响？
        """
        concerns = []
        
        # 检查1：约定冲突
        if proposal.change_type == "amend":
            existing = self._get_rule_history(proposal.rule_id)
            if existing and existing[-1]["proposer"] == proposal.proposer:
                concerns.append({
                    "type": "unilateral_amendment",
                    "severity": "high",
                    "description": f"{proposal.proposer} 单方面修改自己之前创建的规则，缺乏制衡"
                })
        
        # 检查2：Token成本评估
        if "token_optimization" not in proposal.impact_assessment:
            concerns.append({
                "type": "missing_token_analysis",
                "severity": "medium",
                "description": "提案未评估对Token消耗的影响"
            })
        
        # 检查3：认知负载评估
        if proposal.change_type == "create" and not proposal.impact_assessment.get("cognitive_cost"):
            concerns.append({
                "type": "cognitive_cost_unknown",
                "severity": "low",
                "description": "新规则的学习成本未评估"
            })
        
        # 更新提案状态
        review_result = {
            "reviewer": "blue_team_auto",
            "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "concerns": concerns,
            "verdict": "BLOCKED" if any(c["severity"] == "high" for c in concerns) else "APPROVED_CONDITIONAL"
        }
        
        return review_result
    
    def cast_vote(self, proposal_id: str, voter: str, vote: str, reason: str):
        """投票（满意姐、蓝军、Egbertie三方）"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO votes VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (proposal_id, voter, vote, reason, datetime.now().isoformat()))
            
            # 检查是否达到共识阈值
            self._check_consensus(proposal_id, conn)
            conn.commit()
    
    def _check_consensus(self, proposal_id: str, conn: sqlite3.Cursor):
        """检查是否达到通过阈值"""
        cursor = conn.execute("""
            SELECT vote, COUNT(*) FROM votes WHERE proposal_id = ? GROUP BY vote
        """, (proposal_id,))
        
        votes = {row[0]: row[1] for row in cursor.fetchall()}
        
        # 双负责人制度：满意姐+蓝军都同意，或Egbertie一票通过
        # 简化版：2票approve即通过
        if votes.get("approve", 0) >= 2:
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE proposals SET status = 'approved' WHERE id = ?
            """, (proposal_id,))
    
    def enact_proposal(self, proposal_id: str):
        """
        执行通过的提案
        1. 更新COMMAND_INDEX.md
        2. 通知所有相关系统
        3. 写入不可变链
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM proposals WHERE id = ? AND status = 'approved'
            """, (proposal_id,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                raise ValueError("提案不存在或未批准")
            
            # 写入Merkle链（JSONL格式，追加不可变）
            block = {
                "index": self._get_chain_length(),
                "timestamp": datetime.now().isoformat(),
                "proposal_id": proposal_id,
                "proposal_data": row,
                "hash": row[6],  # merkle_hash
                "parent_hash": row[7]
            }
            
            with open(self.chain_file, "a") as f:
                f.write(json.dumps(block, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            
            # 标记为已执行
            conn.execute("""
                UPDATE proposals SET status = 'enacted' WHERE id = ?
            """, (proposal_id,))
            conn.commit()
        
        # 触发系统更新
        self._propagate_change(proposal_id)
        
        return {"status": "enacted", "block_index": block["index"]}
    
    def verify_chain_integrity(self) -> bool:
        """验证治理链的完整性（防篡改检查）"""
        if not self.chain_file.exists():
            return True
        
        lines = self.chain_file.read_text().strip().split("\n")
        prev_hash = "0" * 16
        
        for i, line in enumerate(lines):
            block = json.loads(line)
            
            # 验证链接
            if block.get("parent_hash") != prev_hash:
                print(f"链断裂于区块 {i}: 父哈希不匹配")
                return False
            
            # 验证当前哈希
            # 注意：实际应重新计算，此处简化
            prev_hash = block.get("hash")
        
        return True
    
    def _get_last_block_hash(self) -> str:
        """获取最后一个区块的哈希"""
        if not self.chain_file.exists() or self.chain_file.stat().st_size == 0:
            return "0" * 16
        
        last_line = self.chain_file.read_text().strip().split("\n")[-1]
        last_block = json.loads(last_line)
        return last_block.get("hash", "0" * 16)
    
    def _get_chain_length(self) -> int:
        """获取链长度"""
        if not self.chain_file.exists():
            return 0
        return len(self.chain_file.read_text().strip().split("\n"))
    
    def _get_rule_history(self, rule_id: str) -> List[Dict]:
        """获取特定规则的历史"""
        # 从数据库查询
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM proposals WHERE rule_id = ? ORDER BY timestamp
            """, (rule_id,))
            return [dict(row) for row in cursor.fetchall()]
    
    def _propagate_change(self, proposal_id: str):
        """将变更传播到所有相关系统"""
        # 触发索引重建
        from core.workspace_indexer import WorkspaceIndexer
        indexer = WorkspaceIndexer(str(self.workspace))
        indexer.full_indexing()
        
        # 记录到系统日志
        print(f"🔄 治理变更 {proposal_id} 已传播至所有子系统")

# === 使用示例 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    dao = GovernanceDAO()
    
    # 示例：满意姐提议修改静默模式协议
    proposal = GovernanceProposal(
        proposal_id="",  # 由DAO生成
        timestamp=datetime.now().isoformat(),
        proposer="manyige",
        rule_id="CMD-001",
        change_type="amend",
        from_version="V1.0",
        to_version="V1.1",
        rationale="增加夜间自动进入静默模式的触发条件，减少Token消耗",
        impact_assessment={
            "token_cost": -20,  # 预计节省20%
            "cognitive_cost": 5,  # 学习成本低
            "affected_systems": ["daily_asset_runner", "token_optimizer"]
        }
    )
    
    result = dao.propose_change(proposal)
    print(f"提案提交: {result}")
    
    # 模拟投票
    dao.cast_vote(result["proposal_id"], "skeptor-7", "approve", "审计通过，变更合理")
    dao.cast_vote(result["proposal_id"], "egbertie", "approve", "同意实施")
    
    # 执行
    enact_result = dao.enact_proposal(result["proposal_id"])
    print(f"提案已执行: {enact_result}")
    
    # 验证链
    print(f"链完整性: {dao.verify_chain_integrity()}")

🌪️ 第二维度：反脆弱与混沌工程（Anti-Fragility）

2.1 核心洞察：当前的脆弱性假设

当前系统设计基于“预防故障”的脆弱思维。但43个脚本的复杂性意味着： - 任何预防措施都有失效概率 - 未知的依赖关系永远无法完全消除 - 蓝军的审计可能产生”安全幻觉”

突破方案：主动引入可控混沌（Chaos Engineering）

文件: meta/chaos_injector.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
混沌注入器 - 通过主动故障增强系统韧性
原理：反脆弱（Antifragile）- 杀不死我的使我更强大
"""

import random
import sqlite3
import json
import shutil
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional
import threading
import time

class ChaosInjector:
    """
    混沌注入器：定期、可控地制造小故障，训练系统的恢复能力
    
    故障类型（基于真实风险，但降级实施）：
    1. 数据库锁定（模拟高并发）
    2. 文件权限错误（模拟安全策略冲突）
    3. Token耗尽（模拟上游API故障）
    4. 元数据缺失（模拟外援代码不合规）
    5. 网络延迟（模拟VPS连接抖动）
    """
    
    FAULT_TYPES = {
        "db_lock": {
            "probability": 0.05,  # 5%概率
            "severity": "low",
            "description": "模拟数据库锁定，测试重试机制",
            "recovery_time": 30  # 秒
        },
        "file_permission": {
            "probability": 0.03,
            "severity": "medium",
            "description": "临时修改文件权限为只读，测试错误处理",
            "recovery_time": 60
        },
        "token_exhaustion": {
            "probability": 0.02,
            "severity": "high",
            "description": "模拟Token熔断，测试降级模式",
            "recovery_time": 120
        },
        "metadata_corruption": {
            "probability": 0.04,
            "severity": "medium",
            "description": "随机移除一个脚本的__version__，测试元数据检查",
            "recovery_time": 45
        },
        "network_partition": {
            "probability": 0.01,
            "severity": "high",
            "description": "模拟网络中断，测试离线模式",
            "recovery_time": 180
        }
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "chaos_log.db"
        self.enabled = False  # 默认关闭，需手动开启
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS chaos_events (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    fault_type TEXT,
                    target_component TEXT,
                    duration_seconds INTEGER,
                    system_response TEXT,  -- 系统如何应对
                    recovery_success BOOLEAN,
                    lessons_learned TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS resilience_score (
                    date TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    fault_injections INTEGER,
                    auto_recoveries INTEGER,
                    manual_interventions INTEGER,
                    resilience_index REAL  -- 0-100
                )
            """)
            conn.commit()
    
    def enable_chaos_mode(self, intensity: str = "low"):
        """
        启用混沌模式
        intensity: low(每月1次) / medium(每周1次) / high(每日随机)
        """
        self.enabled = True
        self.intensity = intensity
        
        schedule = {
            "low": 30 * 24 * 3600,   # 30天
            "medium": 7 * 24 * 3600,  # 7天
            "high": 24 * 3600         # 1天
        }.get(intensity, 30 * 24 * 3600)
        
        # 启动后台线程（实际生产环境应使用系统级定时任务）
        def chaos_scheduler():
            while self.enabled:
                time.sleep(schedule)
                if self.enabled:
                    self.inject_random_fault()
        
        thread = threading.Thread(target=chaos_scheduler, daemon=True)
        thread.start()
        
        return {
            "status": "enabled",
            "intensity": intensity,
            "schedule_seconds": schedule,
            "warning": "混沌模式已启用，系统将随机故障以训练韧性"
        }
    
    def inject_random_fault(self) -> Dict:
        """注入随机故障"""
        if not self.enabled:
            return {"status": "disabled"}
        
        # 按概率选择故障类型
        roll = random.random()
        cumulative = 0
        selected_fault = None
        
        for fault_type, config in self.FAULT_TYPES.items():
            cumulative += config["probability"]
            if roll <= cumulative:
                selected_fault = fault_type
                break
        
        if not selected_fault:
            return {"status": "no_fault_this_time"}
        
        # 执行故障注入
        injector_method = getattr(self, f"_inject_{selected_fault}", None)
        if injector_method:
            result = injector_method()
            
            # 记录
            with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO chaos_events 
                    (timestamp, fault_type, target_component, duration_seconds, system_response, recovery_success)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    datetime.now().isoformat(),
                    selected_fault,
                    result.get("target"),
                    self.FAULT_TYPES[selected_fault]["recovery_time"],
                    result.get("response"),
                    result.get("recovered", False)
                ))
                conn.commit()
            
            return {
                "injected_fault": selected_fault,
                "target": result.get("target"),
                "severity": self.FAULT_TYPES[selected_fault]["severity"],
                "auto_recovery_in": self.FAULT_TYPES[selected_fault]["recovery_time"],
                "note": "这是故意的训练故障，系统将自动恢复"
            }
        
        return {"status": "unknown_fault_type"}
    
    def _inject_db_lock(self) -> Dict:
        """模拟数据库锁定"""
        target_db = self.workspace / ".system" / "runtime.db"
        
        # 创建一个锁文件模拟锁定
        lock_file = target_db.with_suffix(".db.lock")
        lock_file.write_text("chaos_lock")
        
        # 设置定时恢复
        def recovery():
            time.sleep(self.FAULT_TYPES["db_lock"]["recovery_time"])
            lock_file.unlink() if lock_file.exists() else None
        
        threading.Thread(target=recovery).start()
        
        return {
            "target": str(target_db),
            "response": "retry_with_exponential_backoff",
            "recovered": True
        }
    
    def _inject_file_permission(self) -> Dict:
        """模拟文件权限错误"""
        # 选择一个非关键文件
        target = self.workspace / "test_chaos_permission.txt"
        target.write_text("test")
        
        # 改为只读
        target.chmod(0o444)
        
        # 定时恢复
        def recovery():
            time.sleep(self.FAULT_TYPES["file_permission"]["recovery_time"])
            target.chmod(0o644)
        
        threading.Thread(target=recovery).start()
        
        return {
            "target": str(target),
            "response": "permission_error_caught",
            "recovered": True
        }
    
    def _inject_token_exhaustion(self) -> Dict:
        """模拟Token熔断"""
        # 创建熔断标记
        breaker_file = self.workspace / ".system" / "CIRCUIT_BREAKER_ACTIVE"
        breaker_file.write_text(json.dumps({
            "triggered_at": datetime.now().isoformat(),
            "reason": "chaos_injection_simulation",
            "auto_release_in": self.FAULT_TYPES["token_exhaustion"]["recovery_time"]
        }))
        
        # 自动恢复
        def recovery():
            time.sleep(self.FAULT_TYPES["token_exhaustion"]["recovery_time"])
            breaker_file.unlink() if breaker_file.exists() else None
        
        threading.Thread(target=recovery).start()
        
        return {
            "target": "token_system",
            "response": "fallback_to_local_mode",
            "recovered": True
        }
    
    def _inject_metadata_corruption(self) -> Dict:
        """模拟元数据缺失"""
        # 在临时文件上操作
        target = self.workspace / "systems" / "_chaos_test_component.py"
        target.write_text("""
# 故意缺失元数据的组件
class ChaosComponent:
    pass
""")
        
        # 定时删除
        def recovery():
            time.sleep(self.FAULT_TYPES["metadata_corruption"]["recovery_time"])
            target.unlink() if target.exists() else None
        
        threading.Thread(target=recovery).start()
        
        return {
            "target": str(target),
            "response": "metadata_validation_failed",
            "recovered": True
        }
    
    def generate_resilience_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成韧性报告"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT fault_type, 
                       COUNT(*) as count,
                       SUM(CASE WHEN recovery_success THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) as success
                FROM chaos_events
                WHERE timestamp > ?
                GROUP BY fault_type
            """, ((datetime.now() - timedelta(days=30)).isoformat(),))
            
            stats = {row[0]: {"total": row[1], "recovered": row[2]} for row in cursor.fetchall()}
            
            # 计算韧性指数
            total_faults = sum(s["total"] for s in stats.values())
            total_recoveries = sum(s["recovered"] for s in stats.values())
            
            resilience_index = (total_recoveries / total_faults * 100) if total_faults > 0 else 100
            
            return {
                "period": "last_30_days",
                "total_injections": total_faults,
                "auto_recoveries": total_recoveries,
                "resilience_index": round(resilience_index, 1),
                "by_fault_type": stats,
                "grade": "A" if resilience_index > 90 else "B" if resilience_index > 70 else "C"
            }

# === 使用 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    chaos = ChaosInjector()
    
    # 启用低强度混沌模式（适合初期）
    print(chaos.enable_chaos_mode("low"))
    
    # 手动注入一次测试
    print(chaos.inject_random_fault())
    
    # 查看韧性报告
    print(chaos.generate_resilience_report())

🔮 第三维度：时间晶体与认知节奏（Temporal Architecture）

3.1 核心洞察：Cron的暴力美学

当前的daily_asset_runner基于工业时代的线性时间观（Cron）。但认知工作有其生物节律： - 深度工作（Deep Work）需要90-120分钟块 - 恢复周期需要15-20分钟真休息（非屏幕时间） - 创造性洞察常在”默认模式网络”激活时出现（散步、淋浴）

突破方案：时间晶体调度器（Temporal Crystal Scheduler）

文件: meta/temporal_crystal_scheduler.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
时间晶体调度器 - 基于认知节律的非线性任务调度
替代Cron的工业时间观，采用认知科学的时间结构
"""

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from typing import Dict, List, Optional, Tuple
from dataclasses import dataclass
import math

@dataclass
class ChronotypeProfile:
    """时型档案（基于Egbertie的生物节律）"""
    deep_work_peak: Tuple[int, int]  # (start_hour, end_hour) 如 (9, 12)
    creative_insight_window: Tuple[int, int]  # 如 (15, 17) 午后低谷后的回升
    administrative_tolerance: Tuple[int, int]  # 可处理琐事的时间
    sleep_onset: int  # 入睡时间
    ultradian_rhythm: int  # 超日节律周期（分钟），通常为90-120

class TemporalCrystalScheduler:
    """
    时间晶体调度器
    
    核心概念：
    1. 时间块（Time Blocking）：基于心流周期，而非任务截止时间
    2. 认知节奏同步：将高Token消耗任务安排在认知低谷期（午后）
    3. 洞察孵化期：在创造性任务后自动插入"发散时间"
    4. 反碎片化：强制最小任务块（25分钟番茄，或90分钟深度块）
    """
    
    # 预定义的认知节律模板（基于研究）
    CHRONOTYPE_TEMPLATES = {
        "lark": {  # 云雀型（早鸟）
            "deep_work_peak": (7, 11),
            "creative_insight_window": (14, 16),
            "administrative_tolerance": (13, 14),
            "sleep_onset": 22,
            "ultradian_rhythm": 90
        },
        "owl": {  # 猫头鹰型（夜猫子）
            "deep_work_peak": (20, 24),
            "creative_insight_window": (16, 18),
            "administrative_tolerance": (10, 12),
            "sleep_onset": 2,
            "ultradian_rhythm": 120
        },
        "intermediate": {  # 中间型
            "deep_work_peak": (9, 12),
            "creative_insight_window": (15, 17),
            "administrative_tolerance": (14, 15),
            "sleep_onset": 23,
            "ultradian_rhythm": 100
        }
    }
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = ".", chronotype: str = "intermediate"):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "temporal_schedule.db"
        self.chronotype = ChronotypeProfile(**self.CHRONOTYPE_TEMPLATES[chronotype])
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS time_blocks (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    date TEXT,
                    start_time TIMESTAMP,
                    end_time TIMESTAMP,
                    block_type TEXT,  # deep_work / shallow_admin / incubation / rest
                    energy_level TEXT,  # peak / moderate / trough
                    scheduled_tasks TEXT,  -- JSON
                    actual_execution TEXT,  -- JSON
                    interruption_count INTEGER
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS task_energy_map (
                    task_type TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    cognitive_load TEXT,  # high / medium / low
                    preferred_chronotype_phase TEXT,  # deep_work_peak / creative / admin
                    min_duration_minutes INTEGER,
                    max_duration_minutes INTEGER
                )
            """)
            
            # 初始化任务能量映射
            self._init_task_energy_map(conn)
            conn.commit()
    
    def _init_task_energy_map(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection):
        """初始化任务类型的认知负荷映射"""
        mappings = [
            ("architecture_design", "high", "deep_work_peak", 90, 120),
            ("code_review", "medium", "deep_work_peak", 45, 60),
            ("deep_insight", "high", "creative_insight_window", 60, 90),
            ("emergence_matching", "medium", "deep_work_peak", 45, 90),
            ("pressure_test_analysis", "high", "deep_work_peak", 60, 120),
            ("metadata_cleanup", "low", "administrative_tolerance", 15, 30),
            ("index_rebuild", "low", "trough", 10, 20),  # Token低谷期运行
            ("ethical_audit", "high", "deep_work_peak", 30, 60),
            ("blue_team_review", "high", "deep_work_peak", 45, 90),
            ("documentation", "low", "administrative_tolerance", 20, 40)
        ]
        
        for task_type, load, phase, min_d, max_d in mappings:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT OR REPLACE INTO task_energy_map 
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (task_type, load, phase, min_d, max_d))
    
    def generate_daily_crystal(self, date: Optional[datetime] = None) -> List[Dict]:
        """
        生成一天的"时间晶体"结构
        不是任务列表，而是能量块结构，任务随后被"倒入"这些容器
        """
        if date is None:
            date = datetime.now()
        
        date_str = date.strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        
        # 基于时型生成基础结构
        blocks = []
        
        # 1. 深度工作块（基于超日节律）
        peak_start, peak_end = self.chronotype.deep_work_peak
        rhythm = self.chronotype.ultradian_rhythm
        
        current = datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=peak_start))
        end_limit = datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=peak_end))
        
        while current < end_limit:
            block_end = min(current + timedelta(minutes=rhythm), end_limit)
            blocks.append({
                "start": current,
                "end": block_end,
                "type": "deep_work",
                "energy": "peak",
                "capacity": "high_cognitive_load"
            })
            current = block_end
            
            # 插入恢复块（如果还有时间）
            if current + timedelta(minutes=20) < end_limit:
                blocks.append({
                    "start": current,
                    "end": current + timedelta(minutes=20),
                    "type": "rest",
                    "energy": "recovery",
                    "capacity": "none"
                })
                current += timedelta(minutes=20)
        
        # 2. 创造性洞察窗口
        creative_start, creative_end = self.chronotype.creative_insight_window
        blocks.append({
            "start": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=creative_start)),
            "end": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=creative_end)),
            "type": "creative_insight",
            "energy": "moderate",
            "capacity": "medium_cognitive_load"
        })
        
        # 3. 管理容忍窗口（处理邮件、杂务）
        admin_start, admin_end = self.chronotype.administrative_tolerance
        blocks.append({
            "start": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=admin_start)),
            "end": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=admin_end)),
            "type": "shallow_admin",
            "energy": "trough",
            "capacity": "low_cognitive_load"
        })
        
        # 4. Token低谷期（午后2-4点，认知最低点，适合自动化任务）
        blocks.append({
            "start": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=14)),
            "end": datetime.combine(date.date(), datetime.min.time().replace(hour=16)),
            "type": "automation_window",
            "energy": "trough",
            "capacity": "machine_only"  # 人类不应干预，纯自动化
        })
        
        # 保存到数据库
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            for block in blocks:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT INTO time_blocks 
                    (date, start_time, end_time, block_type, energy_level, scheduled_tasks)
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
                """, (
                    date_str,
                    block["start"].isoformat(),
                    block["end"].isoformat(),
                    block["type"],
                    block["energy"],
                    "[]"
                ))
            conn.commit()
        
        return blocks
    
    def schedule_task(self, task_type: str, estimated_duration: int, 
                     priority: str = "normal") -> Dict:
        """
        将任务放入合适的时间块（倒入容器）
        """
        # 查询任务特性
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM task_energy_map WHERE task_type = ?
            """, (task_type,))
            task_profile = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not task_profile:
                return {"error": "Unknown task type"}
            
            _, cognitive_load, preferred_phase, min_d, max_d = task_profile
            
            # 查找匹配的时间块
            today = datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT * FROM time_blocks 
                WHERE date = ? AND block_type = ? AND length(scheduled_tasks) < 500
                ORDER BY start_time
            """, (today, preferred_phase))
            
            candidates = cursor.fetchall()
            
            for block in candidates:
                block_id, _, start, end, btype, energy, tasks, _, _ = block
                start_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(start)
                end_dt = datetime.fromisoformat(end)
                available_minutes = (end_dt - start_dt).total_seconds() / 60
                
                # 检查是否适合
                if available_minutes >= estimated_duration:
                    # 更新该时间块的任务列表
                    current_tasks = json.loads(tasks) if tasks else []
                    current_tasks.append({
                        "task_type": task_type,
                        "duration": estimated_duration,
                        "priority": priority
                    })
                    
                    conn.execute("""
                        UPDATE time_blocks SET scheduled_tasks = ? WHERE id = ?
                    """, (json.dumps(current_tasks), block_id))
                    conn.commit()
                    
                    return {
                        "scheduled": True,
                        "time_block_type": btype,
                        "start_time": start,
                        "end_time": end,
                        "cognitive_match": f"{cognitive_load} load in {energy} energy",
                        "recommendation": self._generate_block_recommendation(btype)
                    }
        
        return {"scheduled": False, "reason": "No suitable time block found"}
    
    def _generate_block_recommendation(self, block_type: str) -> str:
        """生成该时间块的工作建议"""
        recommendations = {
            "deep_work": "进入深度工作模式：关闭通知，手机放另一个房间，使用Forest等专注APP",
            "creative_insight": "创造性工作：准备纸笔随时记录，允许思维漫游，准备轻音乐",
            "shallow_admin": "处理杂务：批量处理邮件，不要在此时间段做重要决策",
            "rest": "真正休息：远离屏幕，散步，冥想，补充水分",
            "automation_window": "自动化时间：系统自行运行，人类不应干预，可去午睡"
        }
        return recommendations.get(block_type, "Focus on the task")
    
    def analyze_rhythm_efficiency(self, days: int = 7) -> Dict:
        """分析时间晶体使用效率"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT block_type, 
                       AVG((julianday(end_time) - julianday(start_time)) * 24 * 60) as avg_duration,
                       SUM(interruption_count) as total_interruptions
                FROM time_blocks
                WHERE date > ?
                GROUP BY block_type
            """, ((datetime.now() - timedelta(days=days)).strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),))
            
            stats = {row[0]: {"avg_minutes": row[1], "interruptions": row[2]} 
                    for row in cursor.fetchall()}
            
            # 计算效率指标
            deep_work_interruptions = stats.get("deep_work", {}).get("interruptions", 0)
            total_deep_minutes = stats.get("deep_work", {}).get("avg_minutes", 0) * days
            
            # 效率 = 无干扰深度时间 / 总时间
            efficiency = (total_deep_minutes - deep_work_interruptions * 10) / max(total_deep_minutes, 1) * 100
            
            return {
                "period_days": days,
                "block_stats": stats,
                "deep_work_efficiency": round(efficiency, 1),
                "recommendations": [
                    "增加90分钟深度块" if efficiency < 70 else "保持当前节奏",
                    "减少下午会议" if stats.get("automation_window", {}).get("interruptions", 0) > 0 else "自动化窗口保护良好"
                ]
            }

# === 使用 ===
if __name__ == "__main__":
    # 假设Egbertie是中间型时型
    scheduler = TemporalCrystalScheduler(chronotype="intermediate")
    
    # 生成今天的时间晶体
    crystal = scheduler.generate_daily_crystal()
    print("今日时间晶体结构:")
    for block in crystal:
        print(f"  {block['start'].strftime('%H:%M')}-{block['end'].strftime('%H:%M')}: "
              f"{block['type']} ({block['energy']})")
    
    # 安排任务
    result = scheduler.schedule_task("architecture_design", 90, priority="high")
    print(f"\n任务安排结果: {result}")
    
    # 分析效率
    print(f"\n效率分析: {scheduler.analyze_rhythm_efficiency()}")

🧩 第四维度：暗知识与显性的边界（Tacit Knowledge）

4.1 核心洞察：Polanyi悖论

“我们知道的比我们所说的更多”（We know more than we can tell）。当前系统试图将所有知识编码（docs/、COMMAND_INDEX.md），但这会导致： - 过度显性化：破坏直觉和隐性专长 - 僵化：无法应对未知未知（Unknown Unknowns） - 认知卸载过度：Egbertie的直觉肌肉萎缩

突破方案：暗知识保护区（Tacit Knowledge Vault）

文件: meta/tacit_knowledge_guardian.py

#!/usr/bin/env python3
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
"""
暗知识守护者 - 防止过度显性化导致的认知退化
原理：Polanyi悖论 - 有些知识只能通过实践获得，无法编码
"""

import sqlite3
from pathlib import Path
from datetime import datetime
from typing import Dict, List, Optional

class TacitKnowledgeVault:
    """
    暗知识保护区
    
    机制：
    1. 明确定义"不应被AI辅助的领域"（如：伦理直觉、创造性洞察、关系判断）
    2. 记录"尝试编码但失败"的知识（标记为tacit-only）
    3. 设计"学徒制"练习（强制人类在不看文档的情况下执行）
    """
    
    # 默认的暗知识类别（不应被系统化的领域）
    DEFAULT_TACIT_CATEGORIES = [
        {
            "domain": "ethical_intuition",
            "description": "伦理直觉判断：如'这个数据采集是否越界'的微妙感觉",
            "why_tacit": "依赖文化语境和个体价值观，无法规则化",
            "protection_mechanism": "强制人类做出判断，AI仅提供信息不给出结论"
        },
        {
            "domain": "creative_synthesis",
            "description": "创造性综合：将无关概念连接产生新想法",
            "why_tacit": "依赖于默认模式网络的潜意识处理，无法程序化",
            "protection_mechanism": "在创造性窗口期关闭所有结构化工具"
        },
        {
            "domain": "relationship_nuance",
            "description": "人际关系的微妙动态（如合伙人之间的信任张力）",
            "why_tacit": "依赖于身体语言和情感共鸣，无法数字化",
            "protection_mechanism": "强制面对面交流，禁止仅通过系统记录判断"
        },
        {
            "domain": "strategic_timing",
            "description": "战略时机判断：何时推进、何时撤退的直觉",
            "why_tacit": "依赖于模式识别和潜意识风险评估",
            "protection_mechanism": "决策延迟机制：强制等待24小时后再确认"
        }
    ]
    
    def __init__(self, workspace_root: str = "."):
        self.workspace = Path(workspace_root)
        self.db_path = self.workspace / ".system" / "tacit_knowledge.db"
        self._init_db()
    
    def _init_db(self):
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tacit_domains (
                    domain TEXT PRIMARY KEY,
                    description TEXT,
                    why_tacit TEXT,
                    protection_mechanism TEXT,
                    violation_count INTEGER DEFAULT 0
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS tacit_exercises (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    domain TEXT,
                    exercise_description TEXT,
                    frequency_days INTEGER,
                    last_completed TIMESTAMP,
                    success_metric TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            conn.execute("""
                CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS encoding_attempts (
                    id INTEGER PRIMARY KEY,
                    timestamp TIMESTAMP,
                    domain TEXT,
                    attempted_encoding TEXT,
                    failure_reason TEXT,
                    lesson_learned TEXT
                )
            """)
            
            # 初始化默认暗知识域
            for domain in self.DEFAULT_TACIT_CATEGORIES:
                conn.execute("""
                    INSERT OR IGNORE INTO tacit_domains 
                    VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, 0)
                """, (domain["domain"], domain["description"], 
                      domain["why_tacit"], domain["protection_mechanism"]))
            
            conn.commit()
    
    def register_encoding_failure(self, domain: str, attempted: str, reason: str):
        """
        记录一次"尝试显性化但失败"的经历
        这不是错误，而是有价值的认知边界标记
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            conn.execute("""
                INSERT INTO encoding_attempts 
                (timestamp, domain, attempted_encoding, failure_reason, lesson_learned)
                VALUES (?, ?, ?, ?, ?)
            """, (datetime.now().isoformat(), domain, attempted, reason,
                  "该知识应保持暗态，通过练习而非文档传承"))
            conn.commit()
        
        return {
            "status": "recorded",
            "message": f"已标记{domain}为暗知识域，停止编码尝试",
            "alternative": "设计学徒制练习替代文档"
        }
    
    def check_ai_intervention_boundary(self, domain: str, proposed_ai_action: str) -> Dict:
        """
        检查AI是否即将越界干预暗知识域
        返回阻止信号和建议
        """
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT protection_mechanism, violation_count 
                FROM tacit_domains WHERE domain = ?
            """, (domain,))
            row = cursor.fetchone()
            
            if not row:
                return {"allowed": True}
            
            protection, violations = row
            
            # 检查AI提议是否违反保护机制
            is_violation = False
            violation_type = ""
            
            if domain == "ethical_intuition" and "conclusion" in proposed_ai_action:
                is_violation = True
                violation_type = "AI不应给出伦理结论，只应提供事实"
            
            elif domain == "creative_synthesis" and "structured" in proposed_ai_action:
                is_violation = True
                violation_type = "创造性工作不应被结构化工具干预"
            
            if is_violation:
                # 记录违规
                conn.execute("""
                    UPDATE tacit_domains SET violation_count = violation_count + 1
                    WHERE domain = ?
                """, (domain,))
                conn.commit()
                
                return {
                    "allowed": False,
                    "violation_type": violation_type,
                    "protection_mechanism": protection,
                    "alternative_action": "切换到纯人类模式，关闭AI辅助",
                    "violation_count": violations + 1
                }
            
            return {"allowed": True}
    
    def generate_apprenticeship_exercise(self, domain: str) -> Dict:
        """
        生成学徒制练习（替代文档化学习）
        """
        exercises = {
            "ethical_intuition": {
                "setup": "给出3个模糊的伦理场景（数据在灰色地带），不提供规则",
                "task": "依靠直觉判断对错，然后与导师讨论",
                "success_metric": "能够解释判断背后的价值观（无需逻辑一致）",
                "duration": "45分钟"
            },
            "creative_synthesis": {
                "setup": "随机给出5个无关概念（如'区块链'、'茶道'、'压力测试'）",
                "task": "不借助工具，手写连接这些概念的新商业模式",
                "success_metric": "产生至少3个独特连接",
                "duration": "60分钟"
            },
            "relationship_nuance": {
                "setup": "观看一段合伙人面试视频（仅观察肢体语言）",
                "task": "记录直觉感受，不看 transcripts",
                "success_metric": "捕捉到言语内容与身体语言的不一致",
                "duration": "30分钟"
            }
        }
        
        return {
            "domain": domain,
            "exercise": exercises.get(domain),
            "warning": "这是暗知识练习，不要寻求AI帮助，不要查阅文档"
        }
    
    def get_tacit_protection_report(self) -> Dict:
        """生成暗知识保护状态报告"""
        with sqlite3.connect(self.db_path) as conn:
            cursor = conn.execute("""
                SELECT domain, violation_count, protection_mechanism
                FROM tacit_domains
            """)
            
            domains = []
            total_violations = 0
            
            for row in cursor.fetchall():
                domain, violations, protection = row
                domains.append({
                    "domain": domain,
                    "violations": violations,
                    "health": "🟢" if violations == 0 else "🟡" if violations < 3 else "🔴",
                    "protection": protection
                })
